## Deviations from the approved plan text, already marked "corrected at build" in the plan; adjudicate each
D1. Plan §5 said `onDestroy` gains `route.close` inside the `session?.let`. Built: it does NOT. Reason: at `2ed2cdf` `:1541` `stopRecording()` ends the take and the capture thread's `releaseSession` closes the route with the recorder inside the join at `:1550`; the pre-split `onDestroy` never released the route from there either; a second close from `onDestroy` would race the loop still reading `route.admissible()`. `AudioServiceShapeTest.everyLimbIsClosedWhereEveryEndingReaches` asserts the absence. Say whether the reasoning holds or name the ending on which the route leaks.
D2. `AudioLimbCloseTest` is four rows (one per owner) instead of one `closingTwiceCleansUpOnce`, and it needs `unitTests.isReturnDefaultValues = true` because the owners construct `object : AudioDeviceCallback()`, whose SDK stub constructor throws `Stub!`. The Android value objects come from the stubs by reflection (`stub<T>()`), never asserted on. The cost (an unmocked Android call in any JVM test now returns a default instead of throwing) is disclosed in the gradle comment. Is there a cheaper way to construct the real owners on the JVM without a new dependency, or is this the right trade?
D3. The service field is `warmHoldOwner` (plan) and the owner's own private field is `warmHold`; `WarmHoldOwner.close(reason)` is `warmHold?.end(reason)`, idempotent through `WarmHold.end`'s compare-and-set plus `onHoldEnded` clearing bookkeeping.
D4. A REPRODUCIBLE defect from chunk 1 fixed in chunk 4: the `Buffer sizes:` log line had `read=$PcmAudio.READ_CHUNK_BYTES` unbraced after the constant moved (would print the object then a literal suffix); now `${PcmAudio.READ_CHUNK_BYTES}`. `CaptureBufferOwnershipTest.theRealBufferNumbersAreLoggedRatherThanAssumed` pins the exact line.

## Axes to search (report found / not found per axis, with file:line)
A1. Behaviour parity: for every path in the pre-split service (start success, each pre-session failure exit, capture-loop endings cap/ceiling/error, manual stop, silence stop, `onDestroy` with and without a live take, warm hold start/expiry/device-changed/device-removed/handover/track-failed/keep-alive failure), the same platform calls happen in the same order under the same lock. Name any ordering or lock change.
A2. Lock discipline: which owner methods the service calls under `sessionLock` versus outside it, versus what the original did at the same site. `WarmHoldOwner` methods document "under the session lock"; check every call site honours it (binder `finishTake`, `onStartCommand`, `releaseSession`, `startRecording`'s `handOver`, both `onDestroy` closes).
A3. Thread confinement: nothing new allocates, logs, locks, sleeps or crosses a binder on the capture thread (`captureLoop`, `DetectorFeed.offer`, `PicturePublisher.offer`, `TakeRoute.admissible`, `TakeRoute.markLive`).
A4. Idempotent close: each owner's `close` twice releases each edge once; the `closed` flag placement versus the `finally` blocks in the feeder and analyser threads (double unbind, double interrupt, a late thread nulling a field the next take owns).
A5. Session identity: every lambda the service passes evaluates `session === newSession` (or `!==`) at the time the owner calls it, not captured at construction; `stillWaiting` also reads `isRecording` and the gate.
A6. `TakeRoute.close(keepRoute)`: on the hold path only the listener goes and the `RouteHold` stays live inside the hold; on the non-hold path `hold.release()`. Check `WarmHoldOwner.start` is called BEFORE `closeResources(active, keepRoute = holding)` and that a failed `start` (track cannot play) leaves `holding=false` so the route is released.
A7. Tests: each repointed assertion still names the property it names in its message (not merely a string that happens to exist); `AudioServiceShapeTest.theServiceKeepsOnlyTheSessionAndTheBinder`'s regexes cannot be satisfied by a renamed member (e.g. `warmHold` vs `warmHoldOwner`, `effective` vs `lastEffective`).
A8. Visibility: every new type and member is `internal` or `private` unless a Gradle-module boundary needs `public`.

   160	    /**

   169	    private fun nextCaptureToken(): Long {

   172	            val clock = SystemClock.elapsedRealtimeNanos()
   173	            val next = if (clock > previous) clock else previous + 1L
   174	            check(next > 0L) { "capture token clock overflow" }
   175	            if (tokens.compareAndSet(previous, next)) return next
   176	        }
   177	    }

   252	
   253	        override fun registerSpectrumListener(listener: IAudioSpectrumListener?) {
   254	            this@AudioCaptureService.spectrumListener.set(listener)
   255	        }
   256	
   257	        override fun unregisterSpectrumListener(listener: IAudioSpectrumListener?) {
   258	            val current = this@AudioCaptureService.spectrumListener.get() ?: return
   259	            if (listener != null && current.asBinder() == listener.asBinder()) {
   260	                this@AudioCaptureService.spectrumListener.compareAndSet(current, null)
   261	            }
   262	        }
   263	        override fun getAudioFilePath(): String? = this@AudioCaptureService.lastAudioFile?.absolutePath
   264	
   265	        override fun getElapsedMs(): Long {
   266	            val active = this@AudioCaptureService.session
   267	            // From LIVE, not from the recorder's start: the wait for the earbuds is not the user's time.
   268	            val live = active?.route?.liveAtMs ?: 0L
   269	            return if (this@AudioCaptureService.isRecording.get() && active != null && live > 0L) {
   270	                SystemClock.elapsedRealtime() - live
   271	            } else 0L
   272	        }
   273	
   274	        override fun getMaxDurationMs(): Long = RecordingLimits.MAX_DURATION_MS
   275	        override fun waitForFileReady(timeoutMs: Long): Boolean =
   276	            this@AudioCaptureService.waitForFileReady(timeoutMs)
   277	
   278	        // Legacy method retained for old clients. Audio is now file-backed.
   279	        override fun getAudioData(): ByteArray {
   280	            DebugLogger.warn(TAG, "getAudioData() called, use getAudioFilePath() instead")
   281	            return ByteArray(0)
   282	        }
   283	    }
   284	
   285	    override fun onBind(intent: Intent?): IBinder = binder
   286	
   287	    /** The last binding is gone: clear future pushes; an analyser reference already read may still deliver once. Runs on the service main thread. */
   288	    override fun onUnbind(intent: Intent?): Boolean {
   289	        spectrumListener.set(null)
   290	        return super.onUnbind(intent)
   291	    }
   292	
   293	    /**
   294	     * Started only by [finishTake], to outlive the owner's unbind while a hold runs. Never sticky: a
   295	     * restart after a kill would have nothing to hold, so it ends at once.
   296	     */
   297	    override fun onStartCommand(intent: Intent?, flags: Int, startId: Int): Int {
   298	        synchronized(sessionLock) { if (!warmHoldOwner.isActive && session == null) stopSelf() }
   299	        return START_NOT_STICKY
   300	    }
   301	
   302	    private fun startRecording(
   303	        autoStopOnSilence: Boolean,
   304	        pauseSeconds: Float,
   305	        pick: InputDevicePick,
   306	        keepEarbudsReady: Boolean = false,
   307	        takeId: String,
   308	    ): Boolean {
   309	        // REQUESTED is not the same as VALID AND ENABLED. A pause outside the slider's range reaching
   310	        // this binder means a caller we do not control, so the detector is not built at all rather than
   311	        // built with a number nobody chose. Ordinary recording is untouched either way.
   312	        val validPause = pauseSeconds.takeIf {
   313	            it.isFinite() &&
   314	                it >= SilenceStopDetector.MIN_PAUSE_SECONDS &&
   315	                it <= SilenceStopDetector.MAX_PAUSE_SECONDS
   316	        }
   317	        val detectorEnabled = autoStopOnSilence && validPause != null
   318	        val requestedButRefused = autoStopOnSilence && validPause == null
   319	
   320	        synchronized(sessionLock) {
   321	            // A stopped session remains here until its reader has closed both resources.
   322	            // Starting another take before that point would make the old thread write into
   323	            // the new take's file or release the new take's AudioRecord.
   324	            if (session != null) return false
   325	            lastStartFailure = START_FAILURE_NONE
   326	
   327	            // Route ownership exists BEFORE the session, so every failure path below can release it.
   328	            val audioManager = getSystemService(AUDIO_SERVICE) as AudioManager
   329	            // Filled in once the listener exists; the hold removes exactly this one and no other.
   330	            val routingListener = AtomicReference<Pair<AudioRecord, AudioRouting.OnRoutingChangedListener>?>(null)
   331	            val routeHold = TakeRoute.newHold(audioManager, routingListener)
   332	            // A warm hold hands its route to this take (the link stays up; V13: live at ~120 ms). Any hold
   333	            // that does not match the resolved target is ended by resolveRoute before it sets anything.
   334	            val handedOver = warmHoldOwner.handOver()
   335	            val route = TakeRoute.resolve(audioManager, pick, routeHold, handedOver, TAG) ?: run {
   336	                lastStartFailure = START_FAILURE_NO_INPUT_DEVICE
   337	                routeHold.release()
   338	                DebugLogger.error(TAG, "No input device at all; refusing to start")
   339	                stopSelf()
   340	                return false
   341	            }
   342	
   343	            val nativeBufferBytes = try {
   344	                val minimum = AudioRecord.getMinBufferSize(SAMPLE_RATE, CHANNEL, ENCODING)
   345	                    .takeIf { it > 0 }
   346	                    ?: throw IllegalStateException("AudioRecord buffer size unavailable")
   347	                val coerced = minimum.coerceAtLeast(SAMPLE_RATE * PcmAudio.BYTES_PER_SAMPLE)
   348	                // Whether the floor binds cannot be settled from source: getMinBufferSize is computed by
   349	                // the platform from the device's own frame count. Log both so the answer comes from the
   350	                // phone rather than from an assumption. Android may also enlarge what it actually
   351	                // allocates, which getBufferSizeInFrames reports once the recorder exists.
   352	                DebugLogger.log(
   353	                    TAG,
   354	                    "Buffer sizes: minimum=$minimum coerced=$coerced read=${PcmAudio.READ_CHUNK_BYTES} block=${DetectorFeed.READ_BLOCK_BYTES}",
   355	                )
   356	                coerced
   357	            } catch (e: Exception) {
   358	                DebugLogger.error(TAG, "Failed to determine audio buffer size", e)
   359	                lastStartFailure = START_FAILURE_OTHER
   360	                routeHold.release()
   361	                stopSelf()
   362	                return false
   363	            }
   364	
   365	            var record: AudioRecord? = null
   366	            var output: FileOutputStream? = null
   367	            try {
   368	                record = AudioRecord(
   369	                    MediaRecorder.AudioSource.VOICE_RECOGNITION,
   370	                    SAMPLE_RATE,
   371	                    CHANNEL,
   372	                    ENCODING,
   373	                    nativeBufferBytes,
   374	                )
   375	                if (record.state != AudioRecord.STATE_INITIALIZED) {
   376	                    throw IllegalStateException("AudioRecord failed to initialize")
   377	                }
   378	                val effective = EffectiveDevice(route.reason)
   379	                val takeRoute = TakeRoute(
   380	                    hold = routeHold,
   381	                    resolved = route,
   382	                    effective = effective,
   383	                    gate = LiveGate(gated = route.needsBluetooth),
   384	                    phonePicked = pick is InputDevicePick.Device && InputRouteKind.of(pick.type) == InputRouteKind.PHONE,
   385	                    startedAtMs = SystemClock.elapsedRealtime(),
   386	                    listenerSlot = routingListener,
   387	                    scheduler = routeScheduler,
   388	                    unregisterDeviceCallback = { audioManager.unregisterAudioDeviceCallback(it) },
   389	                    tag = TAG,
   390	                )
   391	                takeRoute.applyPreferred(record)
   392	
   393	                // cacheDir is app-internal and shared by this package's processes. It is
   394	                // excluded from user backups and is less exposed than shared storage.
   395	                val file = File(cacheDir, AUDIO_FILENAME)
   396	                // There is no active session under sessionLock, so no writer can own this
   397	                // path. Remove a partial take left by process death before opening a new one.
   398	                if (file.exists() && !file.delete()) {
   399	                    throw IOException("Unable to remove stale audio recording")
   400	                }
   401	                output = FileOutputStream(file)
   402	                record.startRecording()
   403	                // The route is the target from the first read when the preferred device was set first
   404	                // (V2, 2026-09-16: routedDevice already the SCO source at startRecording).
   405	                record.routedDevice?.let { effective.observe(it.type, it.productName?.toString().orEmpty()) }
   406	
   407	                val newSession = CaptureSession(
   408	                    record = record,
   409	                    file = file,
   410	                    output = output,
   411	                    startedAtMs = takeRoute.startedAtMs,
   412	                    // Allocated HERE, before the thread starts, and never inside the capture loop. The
   413	                    // capture thread may not allocate: it must do nothing that can make it late.
   414	                    readBuffer = ByteArray(PcmAudio.READ_CHUNK_BYTES),
   415	                    token = nextCaptureToken(),
   416	                    detector = DetectorFeed(
   417	                        tag = TAG,
   418	                        autoStop = detectorEnabled,
   419	                        bind = DetectorFeed.bindingThrough(this),
   420	                        unbind = DetectorFeed.unbindingThrough(this),
   421	                    ),
   422	                    picture = PicturePublisher(spectrumListener, TAG),
   423	                    route = takeRoute,
   424	                    keepEarbudsReady = keepEarbudsReady,
   425	                    takeId = takeId,
   426	                )
   427	                takeRoute.registerListener(record, routeHandler) { newSession.bytesWritten }
   428	                session = newSession
   429	                lastEffective = effective
   430	                lastAudioFile = file
   431	                isRecording.set(true)
   432	                terminalReason = TERMINAL_REASON_NONE
   433	                currentAmplitude = 0f
   434	                takePeakAmplitude = 0f
   435	                DebugLogger.startPipeline()
   436	                DebugLogger.mark(TAG, "recording_start")
   437	                DebugLogger.log(
   438	                    TAG,
   439	                    "Recording started (PID: ${android.os.Process.myPid()}, " +
   440	                        "max: ${RecordingLimits.MAX_DURATION_MS}ms, " +
   441	                        "nativeFrames: ${runCatching { record.bufferSizeInFrames }.getOrDefault(-1)})",
   442	                )
   443	                DebugLogger.log(
   444	                    TAG,
   445	                    "route start=${effective.label()} kind=${effective.kind} reason=${route.reason} " +
   446	                        "target=${route.target.label}",
   447	                )
   448	
   449	                if (requestedButRefused) {
   450	                    // The caller asked for auto-stop and cannot have it, which is exactly the state the
   451	                    // notice exists for. Recording itself is unaffected.
   452	                    newSession.detector.markRequestedButRefused()
   453	                    DebugLogger.warn(TAG, "Auto-stop refused: pause $pauseSeconds is out of range")
   454	                }
   455	                if (detectorEnabled) {
   456	                    newSession.detector.start(
   457	                        pauseSeconds = validPause!!,
   458	                        token = newSession.token,
   459	                        takeId = newSession.takeId,
   460	                        isCurrent = { session === newSession },
   461	                        stillLive = { session === newSession && !newSession.stopRequested },
   462	                        endOnSilence = { endTake(newSession, TERMINAL_REASON_SILENCE) },
   463	                    )
   464	                }
   465	
   466	                val thread = Thread({ captureLoop(newSession) }, "AudioCaptureThread")
   467	                captureThread = thread
   468	                try {
   469	                    thread.start()
   470	                } catch (e: Exception) {
   471	                    isRecording.set(false)
   472	                    session = null
   473	                    closeResources(newSession, keepRoute = false)
   474	                    captureThread = null
   475	                    stopSelf()
   476	                    DebugLogger.error(TAG, "Failed to start capture thread", e)
   477	                    return false
   478	                }
   479	                newSession.picture.start(stillLive = { session === newSession && !newSession.stopRequested })
   480	                if (route.needsBluetooth) {
   481	                    takeRoute.watchSink(audioManager, routeHandler)
   482	                    takeRoute.armDeadline(
   483	                        audioManager = audioManager,
   484	                        locked = { block -> synchronized(sessionLock) { block() } },
   485	                        stillWaiting = { session === newSession && isRecording.get() && takeRoute.gate.state == LiveGate.State.WAITING },
   486	                        onRefused = {
   487	                            lastStartFailure = START_FAILURE_EARBUDS
   488	                            endTakeLocked(newSession, TERMINAL_REASON_ERROR)
   489	                        },
   490	                    )
   491	                } else {
   492	                    takeRoute.markLive()
   493	                }
   494	                return thread.isAlive && session === newSession && isRecording.get()
   495	            } catch (e: SecurityException) {
   496	                DebugLogger.error(TAG, "RECORD_AUDIO permission not granted", e)
   497	                lastStartFailure = START_FAILURE_OTHER
   498	                isRecording.set(false)
   499	                routeHold.release()
   500	                closeResources(record, output)
   501	                stopSelf()
   502	                return false
   503	            } catch (e: Exception) {
   504	                DebugLogger.error(TAG, "Failed to start recording", e)
   505	                lastStartFailure = START_FAILURE_OTHER
   506	                isRecording.set(false)
   507	                routeHold.release()
   508	                closeResources(record, output)
   509	                stopSelf()
   510	                return false
   511	            }
   512	        }
   513	    }
   514	
   515	    private fun captureLoop(active: CaptureSession) {
   516	        val buffer = active.readBuffer
   517	        try {
   518	            while (isRecording.get() && session === active) {
   519	                // The cap counts from LIVE; the wait for the earbuds has its own bound in the session owner.
   520	                val live = active.route.liveAtMs
   521	                val elapsed = if (live > 0L) SystemClock.elapsedRealtime() - live else 0L
   522	                if (elapsed >= RecordingLimits.MAX_DURATION_MS) {
   523	                    // The reason is the whole signal. The session owner reads it back through
   524	                    // getTerminalReason and is what tells the user why their take ended.
   525	                    claimEnding(active, TERMINAL_REASON_MAX_DURATION)
   526	                    DebugLogger.log(TAG, "Max duration reached (${elapsed}ms), auto-stopping")
   527	                    break
   528	                }
   529	
   530	                // THE CLOCK CANNOT BOUND THE FILE, because the two measure different things.
   531	                // `record.startRecording()` runs before `startedAtMs` is taken, so the hardware is
   532	                // already buffering when the clock starts, and the check above can pass on its last
   533	                // pass with the file already at the ceiling. One more whole block then goes in.
   534	                //
   535	                // That is not a rounding error. `AsrService` REFUSES a file over the ceiling rather
   536	                // than truncating it, so a few bytes past it discards the entire take: exactly the
   537	                // long dictation this limit exists to keep. So the read is bounded by what is left.
   538	                val remaining = RecordingLimits.MAX_AUDIO_BYTES - active.bytesWritten
   539	                if (remaining <= 0L) {
   540	                    claimEnding(active, TERMINAL_REASON_MAX_DURATION)
   541	                    DebugLogger.log(TAG, "Byte ceiling reached (${active.bytesWritten} bytes), auto-stopping")
   542	                    break
   543	                }
   544	
   545	                // Explicit, because the three-argument overload's blocking behaviour is a default
   546	                // rather than a statement, and this loop's timing depends on it.
   547	                val requested = minOf(buffer.size.toLong(), remaining).toInt()
   548	                val bytesRead = active.record.read(buffer, 0, requested, AudioRecord.READ_BLOCKING)
   549	                if (bytesRead < 0) throw IOException("AudioRecord.read failed: $bytesRead")
   550	                if (bytesRead == 0) continue
   551	
   552	                // Until the gate opens, a read feeds the gate and nothing else: not the file, not the
   553	                // detector, not the picture, not the level. The take's clock starts when the gate opens.
   554	                if (active.route.gate.state == LiveGate.State.WAITING) {
   555	                    val admissible = active.route.admissible()
   556	                    if (active.route.gate.offer(buffer, bytesRead, admissible)) active.route.markLive() else continue
   557	                }
   558	
   559	                val position = active.bytesWritten
   560	                active.output.write(buffer, 0, bytesRead)
   561	                active.bytesWritten += bytesRead
   562	                active.liveVisible = true
   563	                active.detector.offer(buffer, bytesRead, position)
   564	                active.picture.offer(buffer, bytesRead, position)
   565	
   566	                var sum = 0L
   567	                var peak = 0
   568	                for (i in 0 until bytesRead step PcmAudio.BYTES_PER_SAMPLE) {
   569	                    if (i + 1 < bytesRead) {
   570	                        val sample = (buffer[i].toInt() and 0xFF) or
   571	                            (buffer[i + 1].toInt() shl 8)
   572	                        val magnitude = abs(sample)
   573	                        sum += magnitude
   574	                        if (magnitude > peak) peak = magnitude
   575	                    }
   576	                }
   577	                val numSamples = bytesRead / PcmAudio.BYTES_PER_SAMPLE
   578	                currentAmplitude = if (numSamples > 0) {
   579	                    (sum.toFloat() / numSamples) / Short.MAX_VALUE
   580	                } else 0f
   581	                // No allocation, one compare per read: the take's peak, kept for the reader at stop.
   582	                val peakLevel = peak.toFloat() / Short.MAX_VALUE
   583	                if (peakLevel > takePeakAmplitude) takePeakAmplitude = peakLevel
   584	            }
   585	        } catch (e: Exception) {
   586	            synchronized(sessionLock) {
   587	                if (session === active) claimEnding(active, TERMINAL_REASON_ERROR)
   588	            }
   589	            DebugLogger.error(TAG, "Capture thread error", e)
   590	        } finally {
   591	            releaseSession(active)
   592	        }
   593	    }
   594	
   595	    /**
   596	     * Publish the first ending claimed for [active] and stop the loop.
   597	     *
   598	     * The published reason must outlive [releaseSession], because the client polls `getTerminalReason`
   599	     * only AFTER `isCapturing` has gone false, by which point the session is gone. So the value lives on
   600	     * the service while the CLAIM lives on the session, and the claim is what makes it first-wins.
   601	     */
   602	    private fun claimEnding(active: CaptureSession, reason: Int): Boolean {
   603	        if (!active.endingClaim.claim(reason)) return false
   604	        terminalReason = reason
   605	        isRecording.set(false)
   606	        return true
   607	    }
   608	
   609	    /** The user, or anything else with the authority to end whatever take is running. */
   610	    private fun stopRecording() {
   611	        synchronized(sessionLock) {
   612	            val active = session ?: return
   613	            if (!isRecording.get()) return
   614	            endTakeLocked(active, TERMINAL_REASON_MANUAL)
   615	        }
   616	    }
   617	
   618	    /**
   619	     * End exactly [expected], and nothing else.
   620	     *
   621	     * The identity check and the claim happen under ONE hold of the lock. Checking outside it and then
   622	     * ending "the current session" is the shape that lets a detector result from a take that has already
   623	     * finished stop the recording that started after it.
   624	     */
   625	    private fun endTake(expected: CaptureSession, reason: Int) {
   626	        synchronized(sessionLock) {
   627	            if (session !== expected || !isRecording.get()) return
   628	            endTakeLocked(expected, reason)
   629	        }
   630	    }
   631	
   632	    /**
   633	     * Signal the reader to finish. The reader performs the one final native release.
   634	     *
   635	     * Called only while [sessionLock] is held and [active] is still the current session.
   636	     */
   637	    private fun endTakeLocked(active: CaptureSession, reason: Int) {
   638	        if (!claimEnding(active, reason)) return
   639	        // routedDevice returns null once the recorder is inactive, so the final route is read HERE,
   640	        // before stop(); a headset removed just before the stop is then in the record even when its
   641	        // routing callback runs late. A null read preserves the history and proves nothing.
   642	        active.route.observeFinal(active.record)
   643	        try {
   644	            // stop() unblocks a pending read. Do not release here while the reader may
   645	            // still be using the same AudioRecord instance.
   646	            active.record.stop()
   647	        } catch (e: Exception) {
   648	            // The reader's finally block still owns and releases the resources if stop
   649	            // itself fails, so a vendor-specific AudioRecord error cannot leak a session.
   650	            DebugLogger.warn(TAG, "AudioRecord stop failed: ${e.message}")
   651	        }
   652	        DebugLogger.mark(TAG, "recording_stop")
   653	        // The ending is read back from the CLAIM rather than from this function's parameter, because the
   654	        // claim is the owner of the answer and the parameter is only what this caller proposed.
   655	        //
   656	        // Naming it here is what makes a stop button and a silence stop tell apart in a log at all. The
   657	        // other two endings already write their own distinct lines from the capture loop; these two wrote
   658	        // the same one. Ordering against the foreground-service line is NOT a discriminator, because
   659	        // `claimEnding` clears `isRecording` before this log runs, so the session's polling thread can log
   660	        // its own promotion first. Measured on the S26 and recorded in issue #114: a silence-ended take
   661	        // did exactly that, by 11 ms.
   662	        DebugLogger.log(
   663	            TAG,
   664	            "Stopped by ${active.endingClaim.ending.label}. ${active.bytesWritten} bytes " +
   665	                "(${String.format("%.1f", PcmAudio.durationSeconds(active.bytesWritten))}s) -> ${active.file.absolutePath}",
   666	        )
   667	    }
   668	
   669	    private fun releaseSession(active: CaptureSession) {
   670	        var holding = false
   671	        synchronized(sessionLock) {
   672	            if (session !== active) return
   673	            active.route.stopWatching()
   674	            // Capture-loop endings (cap, byte ceiling, error) reach here with the recorder still active.
   675	            active.route.observeFinal(active.record)
   676	            // The one handoff point every ending reaches: a manual stop, the silence stop and both caps
   677	            // may keep the earbuds warm; an error ending and teardown release everything.
   678	            holding = warmHoldOwner.eligible(active.route, active.endingClaim.ending, active.keepEarbudsReady, destroyed) &&
   679	                warmHoldOwner.start(active.route)
   680	            closeResources(active, keepRoute = holding)
   681	            lastSilenceStatus = active.detector.status
   682	            session = null
   683	            if (captureThread === Thread.currentThread()) captureThread = null
   684	            currentAmplitude = 0f
   685	        }
   686	
   687	        // Audio and the PCM file are already closed above. Detector cleanup therefore cannot delay the
   688	        // file becoming ready, which is what the user is waiting for. Nothing here blocks: each owner's
   689	        // close tells its thread to stop and abandons it (#188).
   690	        active.detector.close()
   691	        active.picture.close()
   692	        // Once per take, on EVERY ending (a stop, a silence stop, a cap, a capture error, teardown):
   693	        // release is the one point they all reach. Shape only; `polled` is the proof that no production
   694	        // code polls the picture any more (#187).
   695	        DebugLogger.log(TAG, "Live picture: pushed=${active.picture.pushes.get()} polled=${active.picture.polls.get()}")
   696	        // A hold keeps the service alive; its end calls stopSelf (RULE: the service owns its own end).
   697	        if (!holding) stopSelf()
   698	    }
   699	
   700	    /**
   701	     * The route is released HERE, synchronously, before the session slot frees (the caller clears
   702	     * `session` after this returns under `sessionLock`), so a later start can never install a request
   703	     * that an earlier release still has to clear. The loop is over, so the binder call delays no read.
   704	     *
   705	     * With [keepRoute] only the recorder's listener goes (it dies with the `AudioRecord`); the
   706	     * communication ownership stays in the `RouteHold` the warm hold now carries.
   707	     */
   708	    private fun closeResources(active: CaptureSession, keepRoute: Boolean) {
   709	        active.route.close(keepRoute)
   710	        closeResources(active.record, active.output)
   711	    }
   712	
   713	    private fun closeResources(record: AudioRecord?, output: FileOutputStream?) {
   714	        runCatching { output?.flush() }
   715	            .onFailure { DebugLogger.warn(TAG, "Failed to flush audio file: ${it.message}") }
   716	        runCatching { output?.close() }
   717	            .onFailure { DebugLogger.warn(TAG, "Failed to close audio file: ${it.message}") }
   718	        runCatching { record?.stop() }
   719	            .onFailure {
   720	                if (it !is IllegalStateException) DebugLogger.warn(TAG, "Failed to stop AudioRecord: ${it.message}")
   721	            }
   722	        runCatching { record?.release() }
   723	            .onFailure { DebugLogger.warn(TAG, "Failed to release AudioRecord: ${it.message}") }
   724	    }
   725	
   726	    /** Wait for the capture thread to finish writing and close the file. */
   727	    fun waitForFileReady(timeoutMs: Long = 2_000L): Boolean {
   728	        val thread = captureThread
   729	        if (thread != null && Thread.currentThread() !== thread) {
   730	            thread.join(timeoutMs.coerceAtLeast(0L))
   731	        }
   732	        val ready = thread?.isAlive != true && session == null
   733	        if (ready) captureThread = null
   734	        return ready
   735	    }
   736	
   737	    override fun onDestroy() {
   738	        // Ordered: no hold may start after this flag, so the take stopRecording ends below cannot open
   739	        // one after the route thread is gone (Codex review 1).
   740	        destroyed = true
   741	        synchronized(sessionLock) { warmHoldOwner.close(WarmHold.END_DESTROYED) }
   742	        stopRecording()
   743	        session?.let { active ->
   744	            active.detector.close()
   745	            active.picture.close()
   746	        }
   747	        val thread = captureThread
   748	        if (thread != null && thread !== Thread.currentThread()) {
   749	            thread.join(2_000L)
   750	            if (thread.isAlive) {

    11	 * The recorder's live picture for ONE take: the ring the capture thread offers into, the analyser thread
    12	 * that drains it, the published bands, and the push to the one registered listener (#151, #187).
    13	 *
    14	 * Owned by the take's `CaptureSession` and closed exactly once by the service (#188). The listener slot is
    15	 * the binding's, not the take's, so it is handed in. All of it belongs to THIS take: a thread that
    16	 * outlives its take writes into a dead take's array, and the getter reads the live one.

    19	 */
    20	internal class PicturePublisher(
    21	    private val listener: AtomicReference<IAudioSpectrumListener?>,
    22	    private val tag: String,

    25	    companion object {
    26	        /** Eight chunks of picture backlog, 256 ms: the analyser drains it every wake, so it never fills in practice. */
    27	        const val SPECTRUM_RING_CHUNKS = 8
    28	
    29	        /** The analyser's longest sleep: it re-checks whether its take is over at least this often, unpark or not. */
    30	        const val ANALYSER_PARK_NS = 50_000_000L
    31	    }
    32	
    33	    /**
    34	     * The picture path. The capture thread offers every read here with its position; the analyser
    35	     * thread drains it and publishes into [publishedBands] under [bandsLock], which the binder getter
    36	     * shares and the capture thread never touches.
    37	     */
    38	    private val spectrumRing = BlockRing(SPECTRUM_RING_CHUNKS, PcmAudio.READ_CHUNK_BYTES)
    39	    private val publishedBands = FloatArray(SpectrumAnalyzer.BAND_COUNT)
    40	    private val bandsLock = Any()
    41	    @Volatile private var analyserThread: Thread? = null
    42	    private val closed = AtomicBoolean(false)
    43	
    44	    /**

    46	     * getter. Shape only, logged once at release; `polled` must read 0 in production since #187.
    47	     */
    48	    val pushes = AtomicInteger(0)
    49	    val polls = AtomicInteger(0)

    51	    /**

    53	     * analyser sees the drop as a jump in position and starts its window afresh (SpectrumAnalyzer).
    54	     * **Nothing here logs, allocates, locks or calls across a process.**

    62	    fun snapshot(): FloatArray {

    71	     * constructed or started leaves the take with no picture (the published bands stay zero, the pill
    72	     * shows its resting rail) and touches none of the capture resources. The picture is a limb.
    73	     * [stillLive] is the service's identity check for this take, evaluated by the analyser on every pass.

    80	        }.onFailure {

    83	        }

    87	    fun close() {

    90	    }
    91	
    92	    /**
    93	     * Analyser thread only. Drains the picture ring, analyses every queued chunk in order and publishes
    94	     * once per wake, so what the recorder reads is always the newest audio the ring held.
    95	     *
    96	     * Exits when its take is no longer the live one, when its ending has been claimed, or when it is
    97	     * interrupted, and checks all three at least every [ANALYSER_PARK_NS] whether or not the capture
    98	     * thread unparks it. Never joined: it holds nothing a stop waits for.
    99	     *
   100	     * A failure publishes the zero picture before leaving, so the rail rests rather than holding the
   101	     * last shape it was given, and it costs the picture only: the take does not know this thread exists.
   102	     */
   103	    private fun analyserLoop(stillLive: () -> Boolean) {
   104	        val chunk = ByteArray(PcmAudio.READ_CHUNK_BYTES)
   105	        val bands = FloatArray(SpectrumAnalyzer.BAND_COUNT)
   106	        try {
   107	            val analyzer = SpectrumAnalyzer()
   108	            while (stillLive() && !Thread.currentThread().isInterrupted) {
   109	                var analysed = false
   110	                while (true) {
   111	                    val length = spectrumRing.poll(chunk)

   115	                }

   117	                    synchronized(bandsLock) {

   130	        } finally {

   132	        }
   133	    }
   134	
   135	    /**
   136	     * Analyser thread only. Hand one picture to the registered listener, if any (#187).
   137	     *
   138	     * `oneway`, so this never waits on the app process; the parcel is written before the call returns,
   139	     * so the analyser's own array is safe to pass. A dead client throws: the slot is cleared with
   140	     * `compareAndSet` so a registration that replaced this one in the meantime is kept, and the loss is
   141	     * logged once per take. The picture is a limb: nothing here can reach the capture thread or the take.
   142	     */
   143	    private fun pushSpectrum(bands: FloatArray) {

   145	        try {
   146	            target.onSpectrum(bands)

   148	        } catch (e: RemoteException) {
   149	            if (listener.compareAndSet(target, null)) {
   150	                DebugLogger.warn(tag, "Live picture listener gone: ${e.javaClass.simpleName}")
   151	            }
   152	        }
   153	    }
   154	}

    14	/**
    15	 * The silence detector's feed for ONE take: the ring the capture thread stages whole blocks into, the
    16	 * binding to the detector process, the one thread allowed to talk to it, and the status the binder reads.
    17	 *

    22	 * crosses ownership, ending the take on silence, as [endOnSilence].
    23	 *

    25	 * runs each once. Null [ring] means auto-stop is off: no ring, no feeder, no detector process.
    26	 */

    49	        /** How long the feeder waits when there is nothing to do. It is not the capture thread. */

    54	            context.bindService(Intent(context, SilenceVadService::class.java), connection, Context.BIND_AUTO_CREATE)

    56	
    57	        /** The production [unbind]. */
    58	        fun unbindingThrough(context: Context): (ServiceConnection) -> Unit = { connection -> context.unbindService(connection) }

    61	    /** Null when the user has auto-stop off: no ring, no feeder, no detector process. */
    62	    private val ring: BlockRing? = if (autoStop) BlockRing(RING_BLOCKS, READ_BLOCK_BYTES) else null
    63	
    64	    /** Staging for a read that did not land on a block boundary. Preallocated, like everything else. */
    65	    private val pendingBlock: ByteArray? = if (autoStop) ByteArray(READ_BLOCK_BYTES) else null

    90	
    91	    /**

    93	     *

   303	    /**

   319	                AudioCaptureService.SILENCE_STATUS_READY -> AudioCaptureService.SILENCE_STATUS_LOST_AFTER_READY

   326	    /** Unbinds only this feed's own connection, so a finished take cannot unbind a running one's. */

     8	import android.os.SystemClock
     9	import com.envi.wispr.debug.DebugLogger
    10	import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicBoolean
    11	import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicReference
    12	
    13	/** What `startRecording` needs to know about the chosen route, resolved under `sessionLock`. */
    14	internal class ResolvedRoute(
    15	    val target: InputDeviceCandidate,
    16	    val info: AudioDeviceInfo,
    17	    val reason: InputRouteReason,
    18	    val needsBluetooth: Boolean,
    19	    /** The communication sink selected for a Bluetooth target; null otherwise or when none was found. */
    20	    val sink: AudioDeviceInfo?,
    21	)
    22	
    23	/** A warm hold's route on its way to the next take, with the identity the hold was keeping. */
    24	internal class HandedRoute(val route: RouteHold, val sinkType: Int, val sinkName: String)
    25	
    26	/**
    27	 * The route thread's work, as one owner: post, delayed post and removal on the `AudioRouteThread`
    28	 * handler. Injected so a JVM test can count what [TakeRoute.close] removes.
    29	 */
    30	internal interface RouteScheduler {
    31	    fun post(runnable: Runnable)
    32	    fun postDelayed(runnable: Runnable, delayMs: Long)
    33	    fun removeCallbacks(runnable: Runnable)
    34	}
    35	
    36	/**
    37	 * The route of ONE take (#188): the platform route request ([hold]), what was resolved, what actually
    38	 * captured ([effective]), the live gate, the routing listener, the sink watch and the live deadline.
    39	 *
    40	 * Owned by the take's `CaptureSession`; the service constructs it after the `AudioRecord` exists and
    41	 * closes it exactly once. Session identity and the lock stay the service's: the deadline receives
    42	 * `locked`, `stillWaiting` and `onRefused` and evaluates them where the original did. The routing
    43	 * listener guards on [RouteHold.isReleased]; the sink and `markLive` callbacks only capture this take.
    44	 */
    45	internal class TakeRoute(
    46	    val hold: RouteHold,
    47	    val resolved: ResolvedRoute,
    48	    val effective: EffectiveDevice,
    49	    val gate: LiveGate,
    50	    /** The take asked for earbuds (a Bluetooth target); the phone may then only record if picked. */
    51	    val phonePicked: Boolean,
    52	    /** When the recorder started, so `live after N ms` can be logged. */
    53	    val startedAtMs: Long,
    54	    private val listenerSlot: AtomicReference<Pair<AudioRecord, AudioRouting.OnRoutingChangedListener>?>,
    55	    private val scheduler: RouteScheduler,
    56	    private val unregisterDeviceCallback: (AudioDeviceCallback) -> Unit,
    57	    private val tag: String,
    58	) {
    59	    companion object {
    60	        /**
    61	         * The platform route request, built BEFORE the session so every failure path can release it. Its
    62	         * listener remover targets the slot the take will own, so the hold removes exactly this one.
    63	         */
    64	        fun newHold(
    65	            audioManager: AudioManager,
    66	            listenerSlot: AtomicReference<Pair<AudioRecord, AudioRouting.OnRoutingChangedListener>?>,
    67	        ): RouteHold = RouteHold(
    68	            clearCommunicationDevice = { audioManager.clearCommunicationDevice() },
    69	            removeListener = { listenerSlot.get()?.let { (r, l) -> r.removeOnRoutingChangedListener(l) } },
    70	        )
    71	
    72	        /**
    73	         * Pick the device and, for a Bluetooth target, select the headset as the communication device: the
    74	         * one call that makes Android open the link for `VOICE_RECOGNITION` (measured 2026-09-16: the
    75	         * preferred device alone records silence; the communication device alone starts on the phone).
    76	         *
    77	         * A refusal on the Bluetooth path (no sink, `false`, a throw) does NOT re-resolve onto the phone: with
    78	         * earbuds connected the phone may only record when picked (founder rule 2026-09-18). The take keeps
    79	         * the earbud source with `LINK_REFUSED`, and the live gate's reset and notice speak for it. Returns
    80	         * null only when nothing at all can record.
    81	         *
    82	         * [handedOver] is a warm hold's route: when its sink is the one this take wants, the platform request
    83	         * is adopted untouched and no call is made; otherwise it is released here before anything is set.
    84	         */
    85	        fun resolve(
    86	            audioManager: AudioManager,
    87	            pick: InputDevicePick,
    88	            hold: RouteHold,
    89	            handedOver: HandedRoute?,
    90	            tag: String,
    91	        ): ResolvedRoute? {
    92	            val infos = audioManager.getDevices(AudioManager.GET_DEVICES_INPUTS).toList()
    93	            val candidates = infos.map(InputDeviceCandidate::from)
    94	            val resolution = InputDeviceResolver.resolve(pick, candidates)
    95	            val target = resolution.target ?: run { handedOver?.route?.release(); return null }
    96	            var reason = resolution.reason
    97	            var sinkInfo: AudioDeviceInfo? = null
    98	
    99	            if (InputDeviceResolver.needsBluetoothRoute(target)) {
   100	                val opened = runCatching {
   101	                    val available = audioManager.availableCommunicationDevices
   102	                    val sink = InputDeviceResolver.communicationSinkFor(target, available.map(InputDeviceCandidate::from))
   103	                        ?: return@runCatching false
   104	                    sinkInfo = available.first { it.id == sink.id }
   105	                    val held = handedOver != null && handedOver.sinkType == sink.type && handedOver.sinkName == sink.name
   106	                    if (held) {
   107	                        hold.adoptCommunicationFrom(handedOver!!.route)
   108	                        DebugLogger.log(tag, "route adopt=${target.label} from the warm hold")
   109	                        true
   110	                    } else {
   111	                        handedOver?.route?.release()
   112	                        audioManager.setCommunicationDevice(sinkInfo!!).also { if (it) hold.markCommunicationSet() }
   113	                    }
   114	                }.getOrElse { e ->
   115	                    DebugLogger.warn(tag, "setCommunicationDevice threw: ${e.message}")
   116	                    false
   117	                }
   118	                if (!opened) {
   119	                    DebugLogger.warn(tag, "Bluetooth link refused for ${target.label}; staying on the earbuds")
   120	                    handedOver?.route?.release()
   121	                    hold.releaseCommunicationDevice()
   122	                    reason = InputRouteReason.LINK_REFUSED
   123	                }
   124	            } else {
   125	                handedOver?.route?.release()
   126	            }
   127	            val info = infos.firstOrNull { it.id == target.id } ?: return null
   128	            return ResolvedRoute(
   129	                target = target,
   130	                info = info,
   131	                reason = reason,
   132	                needsBluetooth = InputDeviceResolver.needsBluetoothRoute(target),
   133	                sink = sinkInfo,
   134	            )
   135	        }
   136	    }
   137	
   138	    /** The take asked for earbuds (a Bluetooth target); the phone may then only record if picked. */
   139	    val targetBluetooth: Boolean get() = resolved.needsBluetooth
   140	
   141	    /** The communication sink the take selected, for the one reset and for the hold. Null off Bluetooth. */
   142	    val sink: AudioDeviceInfo? get() = resolved.sink
   143	
   144	    /** Set on the capture thread when the gate opens; the timer and the duration cap count from here. */
   145	    @Volatile var liveAtMs: Long = 0L
   146	
   147	    /**
   148	     * The earbuds this take asked for have been removed (their sink left the device list). Set on
   149	     * the route thread by [sinkWatch]; read on the capture thread. Once true the phone may record:
   150	     * the earbuds are disconnected, which is the one case the founder's rule allows.
   151	     */
   152	    @Volatile var sinkGone: Boolean = false
   153	    @Volatile private var sinkWatch: AudioDeviceCallback? = null
   154	
   155	    /** The route thread's deadline message for this take, removed at every end. */
   156	    @Volatile private var deadline: Runnable? = null
   157	    private val closed = AtomicBoolean(false)
   158	
   159	    /**
   160	     * Only a Bluetooth target, or any explicit pick, names a preferred device; Auto on wired, USB or the
   161	     * phone leaves today's behaviour untouched. A refusal is recorded and the take proceeds on whatever
   162	     * Android routes, reported truthfully by `routedDevice`, never by the target.
   163	     */
   164	    fun applyPreferred(record: AudioRecord) {
   165	        if (!resolved.needsBluetooth && resolved.reason != InputRouteReason.PICKED) return
   166	        val accepted = runCatching { record.setPreferredDevice(resolved.info) }.getOrDefault(false)
   167	        if (!accepted) {
   168	            DebugLogger.warn(tag, "setPreferredDevice refused for ${resolved.target.label}")
   169	            effective.markReason(InputRouteReason.PREFERRED_REFUSED)
   170	            // The link is given back; listener ownership stays with the take so its route changes are recorded.
   171	            hold.releaseCommunicationDevice()
   172	        }
   173	    }
   174	
   175	    /**
   176	     * Registered on the route thread, removed by the hold. A callback checks the hold before writing so
   177	     * one already running when cleanup starts writes nothing; a callback for a dead session finds its
   178	     * own take object, never the live one. [bytesWritten] is the take's byte count for the log line.
   179	     */
   180	    fun registerListener(record: AudioRecord, handler: android.os.Handler, bytesWritten: () -> Long) {
   181	        val listener = AudioRouting.OnRoutingChangedListener { router ->
   182	            if (hold.isReleased) return@OnRoutingChangedListener
   183	            val device = runCatching { router.routedDevice }.getOrNull() ?: return@OnRoutingChangedListener
   184	            effective.observe(device.type, device.productName?.toString().orEmpty())
   185	            DebugLogger.log(tag, "route change=${effective.label()} at ${bytesWritten()} bytes")
   186	        }
   187	        runCatching {
   188	            record.addOnRoutingChangedListener(listener, handler)
   189	        }.onSuccess {
   190	            hold.markListenerSet()
   191	            listenerSlot.set(record to listener)
   192	        }.onFailure { DebugLogger.warn(tag, "Routing listener not registered: ${it.message}") }
   193	    }
   194	
   195	    /** Read the final route while the recorder is still active. Null preserves the history as it stands. */
   196	    fun observeFinal(record: AudioRecord) {
   197	        val device = runCatching { record.routedDevice }.getOrNull() ?: return
   198	        effective.observe(device.type, device.productName?.toString().orEmpty())
   199	    }
   200	
   201	    /**
   202	     * May a read on the OBSERVED route open the gate? An earbud target that Android is routing to the
   203	     * phone may not, unless the phone was picked or the earbuds have left: the founder's rule, applied
   204	     * to the observation and never to the request. A route not yet observed is not refused.
   205	     *
   206	     * The rule binds what this app SELECTS, and is enforced at the gate. A route Android moves by itself
   207	     * once the take is live (V7, a call taking the link) is recorded on the History card ("AirPods Pro 3,
   208	     * then Phone") and not fought: ending a take mid-sentence would lose the words, and capture must never
   209	     * fail (architecture: heart and limbs). Decided at Codex code review 5, 2026-09-18.
   210	     */
   211	    fun admissible(): Boolean =
   212	        !targetBluetooth || phonePicked || sinkGone || effective.currentKind != InputRouteKind.PHONE
   213	
   214	    /**
   215	     * Watch the take's earbuds leave, on the route thread, so the gate can admit the phone once they
   216	     * are gone (V7: Android moves the route itself within 120 ms). Registered after the session exists,
   217	     * removed in [close].
   218	     */
   219	    fun watchSink(audioManager: AudioManager, handler: android.os.Handler) {
   220	        val sink = sink ?: return
   221	        val type = sink.type
   222	        val name = sink.productName?.toString().orEmpty()
   223	        val callback = object : AudioDeviceCallback() {
   224	            override fun onAudioDevicesRemoved(removed: Array<out AudioDeviceInfo>) {
   225	                if (removed.any { it.type == type && it.productName?.toString().orEmpty() == name }) {
   226	                    sinkGone = true
   227	                    DebugLogger.log(tag, "route earbuds removed while ${gate.state}; the phone may record")
   228	                }
   229	            }
   230	        }
   231	        sinkWatch = callback
   232	        runCatching { audioManager.registerAudioDeviceCallback(callback, handler) }
   233	            .onFailure { DebugLogger.warn(tag, "sink watch not registered: ${it.message}") }
   234	        // Reconcile once: a removal between route resolution and this registration is not replayed by
   235	        // the callback (Codex review 5). The list is read AFTER registering, so nothing can fall between.
   236	        val stillOffered = runCatching {
   237	            audioManager.availableCommunicationDevices.any { it.type == type && it.productName?.toString().orEmpty() == name }
   238	        }.getOrDefault(true)
   239	        if (!stillOffered) {
   240	            sinkGone = true
   241	            DebugLogger.log(tag, "route earbuds already gone at start; the phone may record")
   242	        }
   243	    }
   244	
   245	    /** Capture thread, on the read that opened the gate. */
   246	    fun markLive() {
   247	        liveAtMs = SystemClock.elapsedRealtime()
   248	        scheduler.post {
   249	            deadline?.let { scheduler.removeCallbacks(it) }
   250	            deadline = null
   251	            DebugLogger.log(
   252	                tag,
   253	                "route live=${effective.label()} after ${liveAtMs - startedAtMs} ms " +
   254	                    "resets=${gate.resetsUsed} state=${gate.state}",
   255	            )
   256	        }
   257	    }
   258	
   259	    /**
   260	     * The live deadline runs on the route thread as a clock, so a blocked read cannot starve it. First
   261	     * miss: reset the communication device once, if the sink is still there. Second miss: proceed without
   262	     * sound on the earbuds (FORCED), or, when the observed route is the phone with earbuds connected,
   263	     * fail the take rather than record from the phone.
   264	     *
   265	     * [locked] runs its block under the service's session lock; [stillWaiting] is the service's check
   266	     * that this take is still the live one, recording, with the gate WAITING, evaluated inside it; [onRefused] is the
   267	     * service's failure of the take (its `lastStartFailure` and `endTakeLocked`).
   268	     */
   269	    fun armDeadline(
   270	        audioManager: AudioManager,
   271	        locked: (() -> Unit) -> Unit,
   272	        stillWaiting: () -> Boolean,
   273	        onRefused: () -> Unit,
   274	    ) {
   275	        val runnable = object : Runnable {
   276	            override fun run() {
   277	                locked {
   278	                    if (!stillWaiting()) return@locked
   279	                    when (gate.deadlinePassed()) {
   280	                        LiveGate.DeadlineAction.NONE -> return@locked
   281	                        LiveGate.DeadlineAction.RESET -> {
   282	                            val reset = reset(audioManager)
   283	                            DebugLogger.warn(tag, "route reset=${effective.label()} performed=$reset after ${LiveGate.DEADLINE_MS} ms")
   284	                            deadline = this
   285	                            scheduler.postDelayed(this, LiveGate.DEADLINE_MS)
   286	                        }
   287	                        LiveGate.DeadlineAction.FORCE -> {
   288	                            deadline = null
   289	                            if (admissible()) {
   290	                                gate.force()
   291	                                liveAtMs = SystemClock.elapsedRealtime()
   292	                                DebugLogger.warn(tag, "route forced=${effective.label()} after ${liveAtMs - startedAtMs} ms")
   293	                            } else {
   294	                                DebugLogger.warn(tag, "route refused=${effective.label()}: earbuds connected, phone would record; failing the take")
   295	                                onRefused()
   296	                            }
   297	                        }
   298	                    }
   299	                }
   300	            }
   301	        }
   302	        deadline = runnable
   303	        scheduler.postDelayed(runnable, LiveGate.DEADLINE_MS)
   304	    }
   305	
   306	    /** Route thread, under the session lock. Clear and re-select the sink, only while it is still offered. */
   307	    private fun reset(audioManager: AudioManager): Boolean {
   308	        val sink = sink ?: return false
   309	        val stillThere = runCatching {
   310	            audioManager.availableCommunicationDevices.any { it.type == sink.type && it.productName?.toString() == sink.productName?.toString() }
   311	        }.getOrDefault(false)
   312	        if (!stillThere) return false
   313	        return runCatching {
   314	            audioManager.clearCommunicationDevice()
   315	            audioManager.setCommunicationDevice(sink).also { if (it) hold.markCommunicationSet() }
   316	        }.getOrElse { e ->
   317	            DebugLogger.warn(tag, "communication device reset threw: ${e.message}")
   318	            false
   319	        }
   320	    }
   321	
   322	    /**
   323	     * The deadline and the sink watch go, under the session lock, first thing at release: a late
   324	     * deadline or removal callback then finds nothing to do. Idempotent; [close] runs it again.
   325	     */
   326	    fun stopWatching() {
   327	        deadline?.let { runCatching { scheduler.removeCallbacks(it) } }
   328	        deadline = null
   329	        sinkWatch?.let { runCatching { unregisterDeviceCallback(it) } }
   330	        sinkWatch = null
   331	    }
   332	
   333	    /**
   334	     * Everything above, then the route is released HERE, synchronously, before the session slot frees,
   335	     * so a later start can never install a request that an earlier release still has to clear. With
   336	     * [keepRoute] only the recorder's listener goes (it dies with the `AudioRecord`); the communication
   337	     * ownership stays in the `RouteHold` the warm hold now carries. Idempotent.
   338	     */
   339	    fun close(keepRoute: Boolean) {
   340	        if (!closed.compareAndSet(false, true)) return
   341	        stopWatching()
   342	        runCatching { if (keepRoute) hold.releaseListener() else hold.release() }
   343	    }
   344	}
     1	package com.envi.wispr.audio
     2	
     3	import android.media.AudioAttributes
     4	import android.media.AudioDeviceCallback
     5	import android.media.AudioDeviceInfo
     6	import android.media.AudioFormat
     7	import android.media.AudioManager
     8	import android.media.AudioTrack
     9	import com.envi.wispr.debug.DebugLogger
    10	
    11	/**
    12	 * The warm hold BETWEEN takes, as one owner on the service (#188): the `WarmHold` state machine plus the
    13	 * expiry, the two platform listeners and the identity of the sink it keeps warm (type and product name,
    14	 * never id, which changes between reads on this phone). Written under the service's session lock, which
    15	 * comes in as [locked]; the three `warmHold === hold` checks are the hold's own identity and stay here.
    16	 *
    17	 * The one decision that crosses ownership, service lifetime, is [onIdle] (the service's `stopSelf` when
    18	 * no session is open) and [keepAlive] (the service's `startService` so a hold outlives the owner's
    19	 * unbind). The platform edges ([addCommListener], [removeCommListener], [registerDeviceCallback],
    20	 * [unregisterDeviceCallback], [scheduler], [newTrack]) are injected so a JVM test can count what [close]
    21	 * removes.
    22	 */
    23	internal class WarmHoldOwner(
    24	    private val tag: String,
    25	    private val scheduler: RouteScheduler,
    26	    private val locked: (() -> Unit) -> Unit,
    27	    private val addCommListener: (AudioManager.OnCommunicationDeviceChangedListener) -> Unit,
    28	    private val removeCommListener: (AudioManager.OnCommunicationDeviceChangedListener) -> Unit,
    29	    private val registerDeviceCallback: (AudioDeviceCallback) -> Unit,
    30	    private val unregisterDeviceCallback: (AudioDeviceCallback) -> Unit,
    31	    private val newTrack: () -> WarmHold.SilentTrack = { AudioTrackSilence() },
    32	    private val keepAlive: () -> Unit,
    33	    private val onIdle: () -> Unit,
    34	) {
    35	    /**
    36	     * The warm hold between takes, or null. Written under the session lock. The sink it holds is
    37	     * identified by type and product name (never by id, which changes between reads on this phone).
    38	     */
    39	    @Volatile private var warmHold: WarmHold? = null
    40	    @Volatile private var heldSinkType: Int = -1
    41	    @Volatile private var heldSinkName: String = ""
    42	    @Volatile private var holdExpiry: Runnable? = null
    43	    private var holdCommListener: AudioManager.OnCommunicationDeviceChangedListener? = null
    44	    private var holdDeviceCallback: AudioDeviceCallback? = null
    45	
    46	    /** A hold is playing and owns the route. */
    47	    val isActive: Boolean get() = warmHold?.isActive == true
    48	
    49	    /**
    50	     * Under the session lock. A warm hold hands its route to the next take (the link stays up; V13:
    51	     * live at ~120 ms). The identity is read BEFORE the handover: handOver ends the hold, and the hold's
    52	     * end clears its bookkeeping synchronously (Codex review 1).
    53	     */
    54	    fun handOver(): HandedRoute? = warmHold?.let { hold ->
    55	        val type = heldSinkType
    56	        val name = heldSinkName
    57	        hold.handOver()?.let { HandedRoute(it, type, name) }
    58	    }
    59	
    60	    /** Under the session lock. */
    61	    fun eligible(route: TakeRoute, ending: CaptureEnding, keepEarbudsReady: Boolean, destroyed: Boolean): Boolean {
    62	        if (destroyed) return false
    63	        if (!keepEarbudsReady || !route.targetBluetooth || route.sink == null) return false
    64	        if (route.hold.isReleased) return false
    65	        if (route.effective.currentKind != InputRouteKind.BLUETOOTH) return false
    66	        // Exhaustive, no else: a new ending decides here whether it keeps the earbuds warm.
    67	        return when (ending) {
    68	            CaptureEnding.Manual, CaptureEnding.Silence, CaptureEnding.MaxDuration -> true
    69	            CaptureEnding.StillRunning, CaptureEnding.Failure -> false
    70	        }
    71	    }
    72	
    73	    /** Under the session lock. True when the hold is playing and now owns the route. */
    74	    fun start(route: TakeRoute): Boolean {
    75	        val sink = route.sink ?: return false
    76	        val label = route.effective.label()
    77	        val hold = WarmHold(
    78	            route = route.hold,
    79	            track = newTrack(),
    80	            onEnded = { reason -> onHoldEnded(reason, label) },
    81	        )
    82	        heldSinkType = sink.type
    83	        heldSinkName = sink.productName?.toString().orEmpty()
    84	        warmHold = hold
    85	        if (!hold.start()) {
    86	            clearHoldBookkeeping()
    87	            return false
    88	        }
    89	        val expiry = Runnable { locked { if (warmHold === hold) hold.end(WarmHold.END_EXPIRED) } }
    90	        holdExpiry = expiry
    91	        scheduler.postDelayed(expiry, WarmHold.HOLD_MS)
    92	        val commListener = AudioManager.OnCommunicationDeviceChangedListener { device ->
    93	            val ours = device != null && device.type == heldSinkType && device.productName?.toString().orEmpty() == heldSinkName
    94	            if (!ours) locked { if (warmHold === hold) hold.end(WarmHold.END_DEVICE_CHANGED) }
    95	        }
    96	        val deviceCallback = object : AudioDeviceCallback() {
    97	            override fun onAudioDevicesRemoved(removed: Array<out AudioDeviceInfo>) {
    98	                val gone = removed.any { it.type == heldSinkType && it.productName?.toString().orEmpty() == heldSinkName }
    99	                if (gone) locked { if (warmHold === hold) hold.end(WarmHold.END_DEVICE_REMOVED) }
   100	            }
   101	        }
   102	        holdCommListener = commListener
   103	        holdDeviceCallback = deviceCallback
   104	        runCatching { addCommListener(commListener) }
   105	            .onFailure { DebugLogger.warn(tag, "hold listener not registered: ${it.message}") }
   106	        runCatching { registerDeviceCallback(deviceCallback) }
   107	            .onFailure { DebugLogger.warn(tag, "hold device callback not registered: ${it.message}") }
   108	        DebugLogger.log(tag, "route hold start=$label ms=${WarmHold.HOLD_MS}")
   109	        return true
   110	    }
   111	
   112	    /** Runs inside `WarmHold.end` or `handOver`, under the session lock. */
   113	    private fun onHoldEnded(reason: String, label: String) {
   114	        DebugLogger.log(tag, "route hold end=$reason device=$label")
   115	        clearHoldBookkeeping()
   116	        // A new take keeps the service; every other end lets it go once no session is open.
   117	        if (reason != WarmHold.END_NEW_TAKE) onIdle()
   118	    }
   119	
   120	    /** Under the session lock. Forgets the hold's listeners and identity; the hold object itself is done. */
   121	    private fun clearHoldBookkeeping() {
   122	        holdExpiry?.let { runCatching { scheduler.removeCallbacks(it) } }
   123	        holdExpiry = null
   124	        holdCommListener?.let { l -> runCatching { removeCommListener(l) } }
   125	        holdCommListener = null
   126	        holdDeviceCallback?.let { c -> runCatching { unregisterDeviceCallback(c) } }
   127	        holdDeviceCallback = null
   128	        warmHold = null
   129	        heldSinkType = -1
   130	        heldSinkName = ""
   131	    }
   132	
   133	    /**
   134	     * The session owner is done with this take. When a hold is running the service gives itself a
   135	     * started lifetime, so the owner's unbind does not destroy it; the hold's end stops it. False means
   136	     * "nothing to keep", and the owner stops the service as it always did. Under the session lock.
   137	     */
   138	    fun finishTake(): Boolean {
   139	        val hold = warmHold ?: return false
   140	        if (!hold.isActive) return false
   141	        return runCatching {
   142	            keepAlive()
   143	            true
   144	        }.getOrElse { e ->
   145	            DebugLogger.warn(tag, "hold could not keep the service: ${e.message}")
   146	            hold.end(WarmHold.END_TRACK_FAILED)
   147	            false
   148	        }
   149	    }
   150	
   151	    /**
   152	     * End the hold for [reason], under the session lock. Idempotent: a hold that already ended, or no
   153	     * hold at all, does nothing (`WarmHold.end` guards on its own compare-and-set, and the bookkeeping is
   154	     * cleared by `onHoldEnded`). The service calls it from `onDestroy`, twice around its join, as before.
   155	     */
   156	    fun close(reason: String) {
   157	        warmHold?.end(reason)
   158	    }
   159	
   160	    /**
   161	     * The platform half of the hold: a silent `VOICE_COMMUNICATION` stream, which is what Android keys
   162	     * the communication route on (any active playback for the uid). Its own thread paces on the blocking
   163	     * write; `stop()` unblocks it.
   164	     */
   165	    private class AudioTrackSilence : WarmHold.SilentTrack {
   166	        private var track: AudioTrack? = null
   167	        private var thread: Thread? = null
   168	        @Volatile private var stopped = false
   169	
   170	        override fun play() {
   171	            val rate = PcmAudio.SAMPLE_RATE
   172	            val minimum = AudioTrack.getMinBufferSize(rate, AudioFormat.CHANNEL_OUT_MONO, AudioFormat.ENCODING_PCM_16BIT)
   173	            val built = AudioTrack.Builder()
   174	                .setAudioAttributes(
   175	                    AudioAttributes.Builder()
   176	                        .setUsage(AudioAttributes.USAGE_VOICE_COMMUNICATION)
   177	                        .setContentType(AudioAttributes.CONTENT_TYPE_SPEECH)
   178	                        .build(),
   179	                )
   180	                .setAudioFormat(
   181	                    AudioFormat.Builder()
   182	                        .setSampleRate(rate)
   183	                        .setChannelMask(AudioFormat.CHANNEL_OUT_MONO)
   184	                        .setEncoding(AudioFormat.ENCODING_PCM_16BIT)
   185	                        .build(),
   186	                )
   187	                .setBufferSizeInBytes(maxOf(minimum, rate * PcmAudio.BYTES_PER_SAMPLE))
   188	                .setTransferMode(AudioTrack.MODE_STREAM)
   189	                .build()
   190	            if (built.state != AudioTrack.STATE_INITIALIZED) {
   191	                built.release()
   192	                throw IllegalStateException("silent track not initialized")
   193	            }

   195	            built.play()
   196	            val zeros = ByteArray(rate / 10 * PcmAudio.BYTES_PER_SAMPLE)
   197	            thread = Thread({
   198	                while (!stopped) {
   199	                    val n = built.write(zeros, 0, zeros.size)
   200	                    if (n < 0) break
   201	                }
   202	            }, "WarmHoldSilence").apply { start() }
   203	        }
   204	
   205	        override fun stop() {
   206	            stopped = true
   207	            track?.let { t ->
   208	                runCatching { t.stop() }
   209	                runCatching { t.release() }
   210	            }
   211	            track = null
   212	        }
   213	    }
   214	}
     1	package com.envi.wispr.audio
     2	
     3	/** Allocation-only PCM helpers shared by capture and offline ASR. */
     4	object PcmAudio {
     5	    const val SAMPLE_RATE = 16_000
     6	    const val BYTES_PER_SAMPLE = 2
     7	
     8	    /**
     9	     * How much audio one `AudioRecord.read` asks for: 512 samples, 32 ms at 16 kHz.
    10	     *
    11	     * **This is a different quantity from the buffer the AudioRecord is constructed with**, and the
    12	     * two want opposite things. The native buffer is the margin that stops an overrun when the
    13	     * capture thread is descheduled, so it wants to be large. This is the loop's decision
    14	     * granularity, so it wants to be small: the duration ceiling can only fire on a read boundary,
    15	     * and so can a silence stop. Reading the whole native buffer made both coarse to about a second.
    16	     *
    17	     * Android's own guidance is to read in short frequent chunks rather than waiting for the buffer
    18	     * to fill. 32 ms is what the recorder's live picture needs: a syllable is about 100 ms, and the
    19	     * 256 ms read this replaced handed the meter one averaged number per quarter second, which
    20	     * cannot show a voice (#151). The capture service reads with it and the picture sizes its ring
    21	     * from it (#188).
    22	     */
    23	    const val READ_CHUNK_BYTES = 1_024
    24	
    25	    fun durationSeconds(byteCount: Long): Float =
    26	        byteCount.toFloat() / (SAMPLE_RATE * BYTES_PER_SAMPLE)
    27	
    28	    /** Decode complete little-endian PCM16 samples. A trailing byte is ignored. */
    29	    fun toFloatSamples(pcmData: ByteArray): FloatArray {
    30	        val samples = FloatArray(pcmData.size / BYTES_PER_SAMPLE)
    31	        for (i in samples.indices) {
    32	            val offset = i * BYTES_PER_SAMPLE
    33	            val value = (pcmData[offset].toInt() and 0xFF) or
    34	                (pcmData[offset + 1].toInt() shl 8)
    35	            samples[i] = value / 32768.0f
    36	        }
    37	        return samples
    38	    }
    39	}
