    85	        fun resolve(
    86	            audioManager: AudioManager,
    87	            pick: InputDevicePick,
    88	            hold: RouteHold,
    89	            handedOver: HandedRoute?,
    90	            tag: String,
    91	        ): ResolvedRoute? {
    92	            val infos = audioManager.getDevices(AudioManager.GET_DEVICES_INPUTS).toList()
    93	            val candidates = infos.map(InputDeviceCandidate::from)
    94	            val resolution = InputDeviceResolver.resolve(pick, candidates)
    95	            val target = resolution.target ?: run { handedOver?.route?.release(); return null }
    96	            var reason = resolution.reason
    97	            var sinkInfo: AudioDeviceInfo? = null
    98	
    99	            if (InputDeviceResolver.needsBluetoothRoute(target)) {
   100	                val opened = runCatching {
   101	                    val available = audioManager.availableCommunicationDevices
   102	                    val sink = InputDeviceResolver.communicationSinkFor(target, available.map(InputDeviceCandidate::from))
   103	                        ?: return@runCatching false
   104	                    sinkInfo = available.first { it.id == sink.id }
   105	                    val held = handedOver != null && handedOver.sinkType == sink.type && handedOver.sinkName == sink.name
   106	                    if (held) {
   107	                        hold.adoptCommunicationFrom(handedOver!!.route)
   108	                        DebugLogger.log(tag, "route adopt=${target.label} from the warm hold")
   109	                        true
   110	                    } else {
   111	                        handedOver?.route?.release()
   112	                        audioManager.setCommunicationDevice(sinkInfo!!).also { if (it) hold.markCommunicationSet() }
   113	                    }
   114	                }.getOrElse { e ->
   115	                    DebugLogger.warn(tag, "setCommunicationDevice threw: ${e.message}")
   116	                    false
   117	                }
   118	                if (!opened) {
   119	                    DebugLogger.warn(tag, "Bluetooth link refused for ${target.label}; staying on the earbuds")
   120	                    handedOver?.route?.release()
   121	                    hold.releaseCommunicationDevice()
   122	                    reason = InputRouteReason.LINK_REFUSED
   123	                }
   124	            } else {
   125	                handedOver?.route?.release()
   126	            }
   127	            val info = infos.firstOrNull { it.id == target.id } ?: return null
   128	            return ResolvedRoute(
   129	                target = target,
   130	                info = info,
   131	                reason = reason,
   132	                needsBluetooth = InputDeviceResolver.needsBluetoothRoute(target),
   133	                sink = sinkInfo,
   134	            )
   135	        }
   136	    }
   137	
   138	    /** The take asked for earbuds (a Bluetooth target); the phone may then only record if picked. */
   139	    val targetBluetooth: Boolean get() = resolved.needsBluetooth
   140	
   141	    /** The communication sink the take selected, for the one reset and for the hold. Null off Bluetooth. */
   142	    val sink: AudioDeviceInfo? get() = resolved.sink
   143	
   144	    /** Set on the capture thread when the gate opens; the timer and the duration cap count from here. */
   145	    @Volatile var liveAtMs: Long = 0L
   146	
   147	    /**
   148	     * The earbuds this take asked for have been removed (their sink left the device list). Set on
   149	     * the route thread by [sinkWatch]; read on the capture thread. Once true the phone may record:
   150	     * the earbuds are disconnected, which is the one case the founder's rule allows.
   151	     */
   152	    @Volatile var sinkGone: Boolean = false
   153	    @Volatile private var sinkWatch: AudioDeviceCallback? = null
   154	
   155	    /** The route thread's deadline message for this take, removed at every end. */
   156	    @Volatile private var deadline: Runnable? = null
   157	    private val closed = AtomicBoolean(false)
   158	
   159	    /**
   160	     * Only a Bluetooth target, or any explicit pick, names a preferred device; Auto on wired, USB or the
   161	     * phone leaves today's behaviour untouched. A refusal is recorded and the take proceeds on whatever
   162	     * Android routes, reported truthfully by `routedDevice`, never by the target.
   163	     */
   164	    fun applyPreferred(record: AudioRecord) {
   165	        if (!resolved.needsBluetooth && resolved.reason != InputRouteReason.PICKED) return
   166	        val accepted = runCatching { record.setPreferredDevice(resolved.info) }.getOrDefault(false)
   167	        if (!accepted) {
   168	            DebugLogger.warn(tag, "setPreferredDevice refused for ${resolved.target.label}")
   169	            effective.markReason(InputRouteReason.PREFERRED_REFUSED)
   170	            // The link is given back; listener ownership stays with the take so its route changes are recorded.
   171	            hold.releaseCommunicationDevice()
   172	        }
   173	    }
   174	
   175	    /**
   176	     * Registered on the route thread, removed by the hold. A callback checks the hold before writing so
   177	     * one already running when cleanup starts writes nothing; a callback for a dead session finds its
   178	     * own take object, never the live one. [bytesWritten] is the take's byte count for the log line.
   179	     */
   180	    fun registerListener(record: AudioRecord, handler: android.os.Handler, bytesWritten: () -> Long) {
   181	        val listener = AudioRouting.OnRoutingChangedListener { router ->
   182	            if (hold.isReleased) return@OnRoutingChangedListener
   183	            val device = runCatching { router.routedDevice }.getOrNull() ?: return@OnRoutingChangedListener
   184	            effective.observe(device.type, device.productName?.toString().orEmpty())
   185	            DebugLogger.log(tag, "route change=${effective.label()} at ${bytesWritten()} bytes")
   186	        }
   187	        runCatching {
   188	            record.addOnRoutingChangedListener(listener, handler)
   189	        }.onSuccess {
   190	            hold.markListenerSet()
   191	            listenerSlot.set(record to listener)
   192	        }.onFailure { DebugLogger.warn(tag, "Routing listener not registered: ${it.message}") }
   193	    }
   194	
   195	    /** Read the final route while the recorder is still active. Null preserves the history as it stands. */
   196	    fun observeFinal(record: AudioRecord) {
   197	        val device = runCatching { record.routedDevice }.getOrNull() ?: return
   198	        effective.observe(device.type, device.productName?.toString().orEmpty())
   199	    }
   200	
   201	    /**
   202	     * May a read on the OBSERVED route open the gate? An earbud target that Android is routing to the
   203	     * phone may not, unless the phone was picked or the earbuds have left: the founder's rule, applied
   204	     * to the observation and never to the request. A route not yet observed is not refused.
   205	     *
   206	     * The rule binds what this app SELECTS, and is enforced at the gate. A route Android moves by itself
   207	     * once the take is live (V7, a call taking the link) is recorded on the History card ("AirPods Pro 3,
   208	     * then Phone") and not fought: ending a take mid-sentence would lose the words, and capture must never
   209	     * fail (architecture: heart and limbs). Decided at Codex code review 5, 2026-09-18.
   210	     */
   211	    fun admissible(): Boolean =
   212	        !targetBluetooth || phonePicked || sinkGone || effective.currentKind != InputRouteKind.PHONE
   213	
   214	    /**
   215	     * Watch the take's earbuds leave, on the route thread, so the gate can admit the phone once they
   216	     * are gone (V7: Android moves the route itself within 120 ms). Registered after the session exists,
   217	     * removed in [close].
   218	     */
   219	    fun watchSink(audioManager: AudioManager, handler: android.os.Handler) {
   220	        val sink = sink ?: return
   221	        val type = sink.type
   222	        val name = sink.productName?.toString().orEmpty()
   223	        val callback = object : AudioDeviceCallback() {
   224	            override fun onAudioDevicesRemoved(removed: Array<out AudioDeviceInfo>) {
   225	                if (removed.any { it.type == type && it.productName?.toString().orEmpty() == name }) {
   226	                    sinkGone = true
   227	                    DebugLogger.log(tag, "route earbuds removed while ${gate.state}; the phone may record")
   228	                }
   229	            }
   230	        }
   231	        sinkWatch = callback
   232	        runCatching { audioManager.registerAudioDeviceCallback(callback, handler) }
   233	            .onFailure { DebugLogger.warn(tag, "sink watch not registered: ${it.message}") }
   234	        // Reconcile once: a removal between route resolution and this registration is not replayed by
   235	        // the callback (Codex review 5). The list is read AFTER registering, so nothing can fall between.
   236	        val stillOffered = runCatching {
   237	            audioManager.availableCommunicationDevices.any { it.type == type && it.productName?.toString().orEmpty() == name }
   238	        }.getOrDefault(true)
   239	        if (!stillOffered) {
   240	            sinkGone = true
   241	            DebugLogger.log(tag, "route earbuds already gone at start; the phone may record")
   242	        }
   243	    }
   244	
   245	    /** Capture thread, on the read that opened the gate. */
   246	    fun markLive() {
   247	        liveAtMs = SystemClock.elapsedRealtime()
   248	        scheduler.post {
   249	            deadline?.let { scheduler.removeCallbacks(it) }
   250	            deadline = null
   251	            DebugLogger.log(
   252	                tag,
   253	                "route live=${effective.label()} after ${liveAtMs - startedAtMs} ms " +
   254	                    "resets=${gate.resetsUsed} state=${gate.state}",
   255	            )
   256	        }
   257	    }
   258	
   259	    /**
   260	     * The live deadline runs on the route thread as a clock, so a blocked read cannot starve it. First
   261	     * miss: reset the communication device once, if the sink is still there. Second miss: proceed without
   262	     * sound on the earbuds (FORCED), or, when the observed route is the phone with earbuds connected,
   263	     * fail the take rather than record from the phone.
   264	     *
   265	     * [locked] runs its block under the service's session lock; [stillWaiting] is the service's check
   266	     * that this take is still the live one, recording, with the gate WAITING, evaluated inside it; [onRefused] is the
   267	     * service's failure of the take (its `lastStartFailure` and `endTakeLocked`).
   268	     */
   269	    fun armDeadline(
   270	        audioManager: AudioManager,
   271	        locked: (() -> Unit) -> Unit,
   272	        stillWaiting: () -> Boolean,
   273	        onRefused: () -> Unit,
   274	    ) {
   275	        val runnable = object : Runnable {
   276	            override fun run() {
   277	                locked {
   278	                    if (!stillWaiting()) return@locked
   279	                    when (gate.deadlinePassed()) {
   280	                        LiveGate.DeadlineAction.NONE -> return@locked
   281	                        LiveGate.DeadlineAction.RESET -> {
   282	                            val reset = reset(audioManager)
   283	                            DebugLogger.warn(tag, "route reset=${effective.label()} performed=$reset after ${LiveGate.DEADLINE_MS} ms")
   284	                            deadline = this
   285	                            scheduler.postDelayed(this, LiveGate.DEADLINE_MS)
   286	                        }
   287	                        LiveGate.DeadlineAction.FORCE -> {
   288	                            deadline = null
   289	                            if (admissible()) {
   290	                                gate.force()
   291	                                liveAtMs = SystemClock.elapsedRealtime()
   292	                                DebugLogger.warn(tag, "route forced=${effective.label()} after ${liveAtMs - startedAtMs} ms")
   293	                            } else {
   294	                                DebugLogger.warn(tag, "route refused=${effective.label()}: earbuds connected, phone would record; failing the take")
   295	                                onRefused()
   296	                            }
   297	                        }
   298	                    }
   299	                }
   300	            }
   301	        }
   302	        deadline = runnable
   303	        scheduler.postDelayed(runnable, LiveGate.DEADLINE_MS)
   304	    }
   305	
   306	    /** Route thread, under the session lock. Clear and re-select the sink, only while it is still offered. */
   307	    private fun reset(audioManager: AudioManager): Boolean {
   308	        val sink = sink ?: return false
   309	        val stillThere = runCatching {
   310	            audioManager.availableCommunicationDevices.any { it.type == sink.type && it.productName?.toString() == sink.productName?.toString() }
   311	        }.getOrDefault(false)
   312	        if (!stillThere) return false
   313	        return runCatching {
   314	            audioManager.clearCommunicationDevice()
   315	            audioManager.setCommunicationDevice(sink).also { if (it) hold.markCommunicationSet() }
   316	        }.getOrElse { e ->
   317	            DebugLogger.warn(tag, "communication device reset threw: ${e.message}")
   318	            false
   319	        }
   320	    }
   321	
   322	    /**
   323	     * The deadline and the sink watch go, under the session lock, first thing at release: a late
   324	     * deadline or removal callback then finds nothing to do. Idempotent; [close] runs it again.
   325	     */
   326	    fun stopWatching() {
   327	        deadline?.let { runCatching { scheduler.removeCallbacks(it) } }
   328	        deadline = null
   329	        sinkWatch?.let { runCatching { unregisterDeviceCallback(it) } }
   330	        sinkWatch = null
   331	    }
   332	
   333	    /**
   334	     * Everything above, then the route is released HERE, synchronously, before the session slot frees,
   335	     * so a later start can never install a request that an earlier release still has to clear. With
   336	     * [keepRoute] only the recorder's listener goes (it dies with the `AudioRecord`); the communication
   337	     * ownership stays in the `RouteHold` the warm hold now carries. Idempotent.
   338	     */
   339	    fun close(keepRoute: Boolean) {
   340	        if (!closed.compareAndSet(false, true)) return
   341	        stopWatching()
   342	        runCatching { if (keepRoute) hold.releaseListener() else hold.release() }
   343	    }
   344	}

    10	 * Drift guards on the wiring between capture and the detector.

    12	 * Source-level because the subject is an Android `Service` bound across a process boundary, and because

    35	        val end = listOf("\n    /**", "\n    private fun ", "\n    fun ")
    36	            .map { feed.indexOf(it, start + 1) }.filter { it > start }.minOrNull() ?: feed.length
    37	        return feed.substring(start, end)
    38	    }

   194	    @Test
   195	    fun anOutOfRangePauseRefusesAutoStopRatherThanSubstitutingANumber() {
   196	        // A value outside the slider's range arriving over the binder means a caller we do not control.
   197	        // Quietly using 1.5 would give that caller a detector configured with a number nobody chose.
   198	        val start = bodyOf("private fun startRecording(")
   199	        assertTrue(start.contains("it >= SilenceStopDetector.MIN_PAUSE_SECONDS"))
   200	        assertTrue(start.contains("it <= SilenceStopDetector.MAX_PAUSE_SECONDS"))
   201	        assertTrue("requested is not the same as enabled", start.contains("val detectorEnabled = autoStopOnSilence && validPause != null"))
   202	        assertTrue("and the user is told, because they asked for it and cannot have it",
   203	            start.contains("newSession.detector.markRequestedButRefused()") &&
   204	                feedBody("fun markRequestedButRefused()").contains("silenceStatus.set(AudioCaptureService.SILENCE_STATUS_UNAVAILABLE)"))
   205	        assertTrue("no detector is built for a refused request", start.contains("if (detectorEnabled) {\n                    newSession.detector.start("))
   206	    }
   207	
   208	    @Test
   209	    fun losingTheDetectorAfterItWorkedIsSilentButLosingItBeforeIsNot() {
   210	        // Both are "unavailable" to the code. Only one is worth interrupting someone for: a take that
   211	        // never got a detector, versus a take whose recording is still perfectly correct.
   212	        val abandon = feedBody("fun abandon()")
   213	        assertTrue(abandon.contains("AudioCaptureService.SILENCE_STATUS_READY -> AudioCaptureService.SILENCE_STATUS_LOST_AFTER_READY"))
   214	        assertTrue("and the first landing wins, so a second failure cannot rewrite it",
   215	            abandon.contains("compareAndSet(previous, next)"))
   216	        assertTrue("an already-terminal status is left alone",
   217	            abandon.contains("AudioCaptureService.SILENCE_STATUS_UNAVAILABLE,\n                AudioCaptureService.SILENCE_STATUS_LOST_AFTER_READY -> return"))
   218	
   219	        val notice = File("src/main/java/com/envi/wispr/ui/DictationSessionCoordinator.kt").readText()
   220	        assertTrue("only the never-became-available status speaks",
   221	            notice.contains("!= AudioCaptureService.SILENCE_STATUS_UNAVAILABLE) return"))
   222	    }
   223	
   224	    @Test
   225	    fun aStoppedTakeWithNoEndingIsNotTranscribed() {
   226	        // Capture that stopped without publishing a reason has no success to report, and the type says
   227	        // so. Grouping it with the successes sends partial audio on as though it were finished.
   228	        assertFalse(CaptureEnding.StillRunning.transcribes)
   229	        val session = File("src/main/java/com/envi/wispr/ui/DictationSessionCoordinator.kt").readText()
   230	        val terminal = session.substringAfter("when (CaptureEnding.fromAidl").substringBefore("break")
   231	        val failureArm = terminal.substringBefore("CaptureEnding.Manual")
   232	        assertTrue("StillRunning belongs in the failure arm", failureArm.contains("CaptureEnding.StillRunning"))
   233	    }
   234	
   235	    @Test
   236	    fun theCaptureThreadDoesNotLogWhenTheDetectorFallsBehind() {
   237	        // Logging is work, and the capture thread must do none that can make it late. The feeder does
   238	        // the reporting, off this thread.
   239	        val offer = feedBody("fun offer(buffer: ByteArray, bytesRead: Int, position: Long)")
   240	        assertFalse(offer.contains("DebugLogger"))
   241	    }
   242	
   243	    @Test
   244	    fun theReadIsExplicitlyBlockingRatherThanRelyingOnADefault() {
   245	        val loop = bodyOf("private fun captureLoop(")
   246	        assertTrue(loop.contains("AudioRecord.READ_BLOCKING"))
   247	    }
   248	
   249	    @Test
   250	    fun withAutoStopOffNoRingNoFeederAndNoDetectorProcess() {
   251	        // The switch off must be exactly today's behaviour, not today's behaviour plus idle machinery.
   252	        val start = bodyOf("private fun startRecording(")
   253	        // detectorEnabled requires autoStopOnSilence, so the switch being off is sufficient on its own.
   254	        assertTrue(start.contains("val detectorEnabled = autoStopOnSilence &&"))
   255	        assertTrue(start.contains("if (detectorEnabled) {\n                    newSession.detector.start("))
   256	        assertTrue("the feed is told the switch's state at construction", start.contains("autoStop = detectorEnabled,"))
   257	        assertTrue(feed.contains("private val ring: BlockRing? = if (autoStop) BlockRing(RING_BLOCKS, READ_BLOCK_BYTES) else null"))
   258	        assertTrue(feed.contains("private val pendingBlock: ByteArray? = if (autoStop) ByteArray(READ_BLOCK_BYTES) else null"))
   259	    }
   260	
   261	    @Test
   262	    fun theStopLineSaysHowTheTakeEnded() {
   263	        // Two endings wrote the same line: a stop button and a silence stop. That is the ONE question a
   264	        // support report or a hardware run asks of this log, and it could not be answered from it.
   265	        val end = bodyOf("private fun endTakeLocked(")
   266	        assertTrue(
   267	            "the stop line must name the ending",
   268	            end.contains("\"Stopped by ${'$'}{active.endingClaim.ending.label}."),
   269	        )
   270	        // Read from the CLAIM, not from this function's `reason` parameter. The claim is first-wins and
   271	        // is the owner of the answer; the parameter is only what one caller proposed.
   272	        assertFalse(
   273	            "the line must not name the parameter instead of the claimed ending",
   274	            end.contains("CaptureEnding.fromAidl(reason)"),
   275	        )
   276	    }
   277	
   278	    @Test
   279	    fun theTwoEndingsThatDoNotReachTheStopLineStillLogSomethingOfTheirOwn() {
   280	        // Max duration and a capture failure claim their ending inside the loop and break, so they never
   281	        // reach endTakeLocked. They are already distinguishable, and this pins that they stay so: without
   282	        // it, naming the ending in one place reads as covering all four.
   283	        val loop = bodyOf("private fun captureLoop(")
   284	        assertTrue(loop.contains("claimEnding(active, TERMINAL_REASON_MAX_DURATION)"))
   285	        assertTrue(loop.contains("Max duration reached"))
   286	        assertTrue(loop.contains("claimEnding(active, TERMINAL_REASON_ERROR)"))
   287	        assertTrue(loop.contains("Capture thread error"))
   288	    }
   289	}
     1	package com.envi.wispr.audio
     2	
     3	import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
     4	import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
     5	import org.junit.Test
     6	import java.io.File
     7	
     8	/**
     9	 * How the gate and the hold are wired into the two services, read from the source like the other
    10	 * wiring guards (the capture loop is a thread inside an Android Service and has no unit seam).
    11	 * Each assertion names the property that a plausible edit would silently remove.
    12	 */
    13	class LiveGateWiringTest {
    14	    private val capture = File("src/main/java/com/envi/wispr/audio/AudioCaptureService.kt").readText()
    15	    /** Since #188 the route (gate, deadline, sink watch) and the warm hold are owners of their own. */
    16	    private val route = File("src/main/java/com/envi/wispr/audio/TakeRoute.kt").readText()
    17	    private val hold = File("src/main/java/com/envi/wispr/audio/WarmHoldOwner.kt").readText()
    18	    /**
    19	     * Since #186 the owner is the coordinator; the three connections live in `PipelineBindings` and the
    20	     * preference writes in `SessionPreferencesSource`. Each pin below reads the file its statement moved to.
    21	     */
    22	    private val session = File("src/main/java/com/envi/wispr/ui/DictationSessionCoordinator.kt").readText()
    23	    private val preferences = File("src/main/java/com/envi/wispr/ui/SessionPreferencesSource.kt").readText()
    24	    private val bindings = File("src/main/java/com/envi/wispr/ui/PipelineBindings.kt").readText()
    25	
    26	    private fun body(source: String, head: String): String =
    27	        source.substringAfter(head).substringBefore("\n    private fun ").substringBefore("\n    override fun ")
    28	
    29	    /** One function of an owner class, where every member carries its own KDoc and most are public. */
    30	    private fun owned(source: String, head: String): String {
    31	        assertTrue("$head must exist", source.contains(head))
    32	        return source.substringAfter(head).substringBefore("\n    /**").substringBefore("\n    private fun ").substringBefore("\n    fun ")
    33	    }
    34	
    35	    @Test
    36	    fun bytesBeforeLiveAreNotWrittenAndFeedNothingButTheGate() {
    37	        val loop = body(capture, "private fun captureLoop(")
    38	        val gateCheck = loop.indexOf("if (active.route.gate.state == LiveGate.State.WAITING)")
    39	        val write = loop.indexOf("active.output.write(buffer, 0, bytesRead)")
    40	        val detector = loop.indexOf("active.detector.offer(buffer, bytesRead, position)")
    41	        val spectrum = loop.indexOf("active.picture.offer(")
    42	        val amplitude = loop.indexOf("currentAmplitude =")
    43	        assertTrue("the gate is consulted before the write", gateCheck in 0 until write)
    44	        assertTrue("a waiting read skips the rest of the loop", loop.substring(gateCheck, write).contains("else continue"))
    45	        assertTrue("the detector, the picture and the level all sit after the write", detector > write && spectrum > write && amplitude > write)
    46	        assertTrue("the first written block makes the verdict visible", loop.indexOf("active.liveVisible = true") > write)
    47	    }
    48	
    49	    @Test
    50	    fun theBinderReportsReadyOnlyAfterTheFirstSavedByte() {
    51	        val getter = body(capture, "override fun getLiveState(): Int {")
    52	        assertTrue(getter.contains("if (!active.liveVisible) return LIVE_WAITING"))
    53	        val elapsed = body(capture, "override fun getElapsedMs(): Long {")
    54	        assertTrue("the timer counts from live", elapsed.contains("active?.route?.liveAtMs") && !elapsed.contains("startedAtMs"))
    55	    }
    56	
    57	    @Test
    58	    fun theDeadlineIsAClockOnTheRouteThreadAndResetsOnlyWhileTheSinkIsOffered() {
    59	        val arm = owned(route, "fun armDeadline(")
    60	        assertTrue(arm.contains("scheduler.postDelayed(runnable, LiveGate.DEADLINE_MS)"))
    61	        assertTrue("the second deadline is re-armed after the reset", arm.contains("scheduler.postDelayed(this, LiveGate.DEADLINE_MS)"))
    62	        assertTrue("the scheduler is the service's route thread", capture.contains("override fun postDelayed(runnable: Runnable, delayMs: Long) { routeHandler.postDelayed(runnable, delayMs) }"))
    63	        assertTrue("forcing asks whether the observed route may be forced", arm.contains("if (admissible())"))
    64	        assertTrue("an earbud target routed to the phone refuses instead", arm.contains("onRefused()"))
    65	        val start = body(capture, "private fun startRecording(")
    66	        assertTrue("and the service turns the refusal into a failed start", start.substringAfter("onRefused = {").substringBefore("}").contains("lastStartFailure = START_FAILURE_EARBUDS"))
    67	        val reset = owned(route, "private fun reset(audioManager: AudioManager): Boolean")
    68	        assertTrue(reset.contains("availableCommunicationDevices.any"))
    69	        assertTrue(reset.contains("clearCommunicationDevice()") && reset.contains("setCommunicationDevice(sink)"))
    70	    }
    71	
    72	    @Test
    73	    fun aRefusedLinkNoLongerResolvesOntoThePhone() {
    74	        val resolve = route.substringAfter("fun resolve(").substringBefore("\n    }\n")
    75	        assertFalse(resolve.contains("allowBluetooth = false"))
    76	        assertTrue(resolve.contains("reason = InputRouteReason.LINK_REFUSED"))
    77	    }
    78	
    79	    @Test
    80	    fun theHoldStartsFromTheOneReleasePathAndOnlyForFinishedTakes() {
    81	        val release = body(capture, "private fun releaseSession(")
    82	        assertTrue(release.contains("holding = warmHoldOwner.eligible(active.route, active.endingClaim.ending, active.keepEarbudsReady, destroyed) &&\n                warmHoldOwner.start(active.route)"))
    83	        assertTrue(release.contains("closeResources(active, keepRoute = holding)"))
    84	        assertTrue("a hold keeps the service alive", release.contains("if (!holding) stopSelf()"))
    85	        val eligible = owned(hold, "fun eligible(")
    86	        assertTrue(eligible.contains("CaptureEnding.Manual, CaptureEnding.Silence, CaptureEnding.MaxDuration -> true"))
    87	        assertTrue(eligible.contains("CaptureEnding.StillRunning, CaptureEnding.Failure -> false"))
    88	        assertTrue("the setting is read from the take, frozen at start", eligible.contains("if (!keepEarbudsReady || !route.targetBluetooth || route.sink == null) return false"))
    89	        assertTrue("only a take that ended on Bluetooth holds", eligible.contains("route.effective.currentKind != InputRouteKind.BLUETOOTH) return false"))
    90	    }
    91	
    92	    @Test
    93	    fun finishTakeGivesTheServiceAStartedLifetimeAndTheStartIsNotSticky() {
    94	        val finish = owned(hold, "fun finishTake(): Boolean {")
    95	        assertTrue("the hold keeps the service through the injected edge", finish.contains("keepAlive()"))
    96	        assertTrue("which is a started lifetime", capture.contains("keepAlive = { startService(Intent(this, AudioCaptureService::class.java)) }"))
    97	        assertTrue("and the binder calls it under the session lock", capture.contains("override fun finishTake(): Boolean = synchronized(sessionLock) { this@AudioCaptureService.warmHoldOwner.finishTake() }"))
    98	        val start = body(capture, "override fun onStartCommand(")
    99	        assertTrue(start.contains("return START_NOT_STICKY"))
   100	        assertTrue(start.contains("if (!warmHoldOwner.isActive && session == null) stopSelf()"))
   101	        assertTrue(capture.contains("synchronized(sessionLock) { warmHoldOwner.close(WarmHold.END_DESTROYED) }"))
   102	        assertTrue("the owner's close is the hold's end", owned(hold, "fun close(reason: String)").contains("warmHold?.end(reason)"))
   103	        val destroy = capture.substringAfter("override fun onDestroy()")
   104	        assertTrue("the flag precedes the stop that could end a take", destroy.indexOf("destroyed = true") < destroy.indexOf("stopRecording()"))
   105	        assertTrue("no hold may start after teardown began", owned(hold, "fun eligible(").contains("if (destroyed) return false"))
   106	        assertTrue("a hold started before the join is ended before the route thread quits",
   107	            destroy.lastIndexOf("warmHoldOwner.close(WarmHold.END_DESTROYED)") < destroy.indexOf("routeThread.quitSafely()"))
   108	    }
   109	
   110	    @Test
   111	    fun removedEarbudsAdmitThePhoneAndADeadAudioProcessEndsAStartingTake() {
   112	        val admissible = owned(route, "fun admissible(): Boolean =")
   113	        assertTrue("disconnected earbuds are the one case the phone may record", admissible.contains("sinkGone"))
   114	        val start = body(capture, "private fun startRecording(")
   115	        assertTrue(start.contains("takeRoute.watchSink(audioManager, routeHandler)"))
   116	        val watch = owned(route, "fun watchSink(")
   117	        assertTrue("the device list is reconciled after registering, so a removal in between is not missed",
   118	            watch.indexOf("registerAudioDeviceCallback") < watch.indexOf("availableCommunicationDevices.any"))
   119	        assertTrue("release stops the watch first", body(capture, "private fun releaseSession(").contains("active.route.stopWatching()"))
   120	        assertTrue("and stopping it unregisters the callback", owned(route, "fun stopWatching()").contains("unregisterDeviceCallback(it)"))
   121	        val disconnect = body(session, "override fun onCaptureDisconnected()")
   122	        assertTrue(disconnect.contains("seen == SessionState.RECORDING || seen == SessionState.STARTING"))
   123	        // The link is cleared BEFORE the owner hears of the death, as the proxy field was (Codex review C1).
   124	        val clear = bindings.indexOf("capture = null")
   125	        val notify = bindings.indexOf("listener?.onCaptureDisconnected()")
   126	        assertTrue("the capture link must be cleared before the disconnect listener runs", clear >= 0 && notify > clear)
   127	    }
   128	
   129	    @Test
   130	    fun everyTransitionOutOfStartingOrRecordingTakesThePublishLock() {
   131	        // The producers of the race class Codex review 3 named: enumerated from the CAS sites, not
   132	        // from the findings. A new CAS out of STARTING or RECORDING outside the lock fails here.
   133	        val casLines = Regex("compareAndSet\\(SessionState\\.(STARTING|RECORDING), SessionState\\.\\w+\\)").findAll(session).count()
   134	        val lockedCas = Regex("synchronized\\(publishLock\\) \\{\\s*\\n\\s*if \\(!state\\.compareAndSet\\(SessionState\\.(STARTING|RECORDING), SessionState\\.\\w+\\)\\)").findAll(session).count()
   135	        assertTrue("every CAS out of STARTING/RECORDING ($casLines) sits under publishLock ($lockedCas)", casLines == lockedCas)
   136	        val wait = body(session, "private fun waitForLive()")
   137	        assertTrue("publication is posted to the main thread, where commands are dispatched", wait.contains("host.postToMain { publishLive(forced) }"))
   138	        assertTrue(body(session, "private fun publishLive(").contains("check(host.onMainThread())"))
   139	        assertTrue("the waiter claims failure before any cleanup", wait.indexOf("failWhileStarting(") < wait.indexOf("waitForFileReady"))
   140	        assertFalse("the waiter never overwrites another owner with showError", wait.contains("showError("))
   141	    }
   142	
   143	    @Test
   144	    fun theHeldIdentityIsReadBeforeTheHandoverClearsIt() {

   148	        assertTrue(read in 0 until hand)
   149	        assertTrue("the service takes the handed route from the owner", body(capture, "private fun startRecording(").contains("val handedOver = warmHoldOwner.handOver()"))
   150	        val resolve = route.substringAfter("fun resolve(").substringBefore("\n    }\n")
   151	        assertTrue(resolve.contains("handedOver.sinkType == sink.type && handedOver.sinkName == sink.name"))
   152	    }

   154	    @Test

   159	            assertTrue("the start call carries $it", start.substringAfter("startCaptureForTake(").substringBefore(")").contains(it))

     1	package com.envi.wispr.audio
     2	
     3	import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
     4	import org.junit.Test
     5	import java.io.File
     6	
     7	/**
     8	 * Drift Guard, not product coverage.
     9	 *
    10	 * The defect this exists for is the one issue #44 recorded: the level was computed correctly for months
    11	 * and no route carried it to the screen, so every part looked right on its own. Each link of the route
    12	 * from the audio process to the rail is asserted here because the broken state compiles, passes every
    13	 * other test, and shows nothing. #151 replaced the scalar level with a per-band picture and the polling
    14	 * tick's meter step with a thread of its own; #187 replaced that thread's thirty polls a second with one
    15	 * picture pushed per analyser wake over a oneway listener; the links changed, the reason for guarding
    16	 * them did not.
    17	 *
    18	 * Source-level because the two ends are an Android `Service` and a `View` attached to a `WindowManager`,
    19	 * neither of which a JVM test can stand up.
    20	 */
    21	class LiveAudioMeterWiringTest {
    22	
    23	    private val capture = File("src/main/java/com/envi/wispr/audio/AudioCaptureService.kt").readText()
    24	    /** The picture's owner since #188: ring, analyser thread, published bands and the push. */
    25	    private val picture = File("src/main/java/com/envi/wispr/audio/PicturePublisher.kt").readText()
    26	    /** The owner since #186: the meter moved from the Service to the coordinator with its seams (`surface` over the overlay state). */
    27	    private val session = File("src/main/java/com/envi/wispr/ui/DictationSessionCoordinator.kt").readText()
    28	    private val recorder = File("src/main/java/com/envi/wispr/ui/RecorderSurface.kt").readText()
    29	    private val overlayState = File("src/main/java/com/envi/wispr/shortcuts/RecordingOverlayState.kt").readText()
    30	    private val overlay = File("src/main/java/com/envi/wispr/paste/RecordingAccessibilityOverlay.kt").readText()
    31	    private val meterView = File("src/main/java/com/envi/wispr/paste/RecordingLevelMeterView.kt").readText()
    32	    private val aidl = File("src/main/aidl/com/envi/wispr/audio/IAudioCaptureService.aidl").readText()
    33	    private val listenerAidl = File("src/main/aidl/com/envi/wispr/audio/IAudioSpectrumListener.aidl").readText()
    34	    private val bindings = File("src/main/java/com/envi/wispr/ui/PipelineBindings.kt").readText()
    35	
    36	    /** The text of one named function, and a loud failure if the name is gone. */
    37	    private fun body(source: String, declaration: String): String {
    38	        assertTrue("$declaration must exist; a renamed function would otherwise be inspected as unrelated text", source.contains(declaration))
    39	        return source.substringAfter(declaration).substringBefore("\n    /**")
    40	    }
    41	
    42	    @Test
    43	    fun theOwnerSubscribesToThePictureAndNeverPollsIt() {
    44	        val listen = body(session, "private fun listenForPicture()")
    45	        assertTrue("the serial is captured once, when the take starts", listen.contains("val takeSerial = surface.currentTakeSerial()"))
    46	        assertTrue(
    47	            "the owner registers a listener that stamps that serial on every picture",
    48	            listen.contains("pipeline.capture?.listenForSpectrum { bands -> surface.updateBands(takeSerial, bands) }"),
    49	        )
    50	        assertTrue("and registering cannot end the take", listen.contains("runCatching {\n            pipeline.capture?.listenForSpectrum"))
    51	        // The seam is only as good as its production delegate (Codex review C1, 2026-09-20).
    52	        assertTrue(recorder.contains("override fun updateBands(takeSerial: Long, bands: FloatArray) = RecordingOverlayState.updateBands(takeSerial, bands)"))
    53	        assertTrue(recorder.contains("override fun currentTakeSerial(): Long = RecordingOverlayState.snapshots.value.takeSerial"))
    54	        val polling = body(session, "private fun startPolling()")
    55	        assertTrue("the polling thread registers the listener once the take is live", polling.contains("listenForPicture()"))
    56	        assertTrue("and no meter thread remains", !session.contains("DictationMeterThread") && !session.contains("METER_INTERVAL_MS"))
    57	    }
    58	
    59	    @Test
    60	    fun theOwnerUnsubscribesWhereEverySessionEnds() {
    61	        val finish = body(session, "private fun finishSession()")
    62	        val unsubscribe = finish.indexOf("runCatching { pipeline.capture?.stopListeningForSpectrum() }")
    63	        val unbind = finish.indexOf("pipeline.unbind()")
    64	        assertTrue("finishSession unregisters the listener", unsubscribe >= 0)
    65	        assertTrue("before it unbinds", unbind > unsubscribe)
    66	    }
    67	
    68	    @Test
    69	    fun nothingInProductionReadsThePicture() {
    70	        // architecture-rules.md RULE: no-idle-cost — surfaces are pushed a picture, they never poll for
    71	        // one. Since #187 nothing polls: the only mention of the legacy getter in production is the Stub
    72	        // override that keeps the transaction alive. Repository-wide, so a poller reintroduced anywhere
    73	        // (the proxy, a screen, a service) turns this red, not only one in the owner.
    74	        val production = File("src/main/java").walkTopDown().filter { it.isFile && it.extension == "kt" }.toList()
    75	        assertTrue("the production tree must be readable", production.size > 100)
    76	        val readers = production.filter { file ->
    77	            val text = file.readText()
    78	            text.contains("spectrumBands") || text.contains("getSpectrumBands")
    79	        }.map { it.path }
    80	        assertTrue(

   103	    @Test
   104	    fun theAnalyserPushesOutsideTheLockAndNeverFromCapture() {
   105	        // A sequential test cannot open the window between a half-written picture and its reader
   106	        // (validation-discipline.md RULE: a-single-threaded-test-cannot-distinguish-atomic-from-check-then-act),
   107	        // so the lock is asserted at the source: the copy in and the copy out both sit inside it, and
   108	        // the push (a binder transaction) sits after the lock closes.
   109	        val loop = body(picture, "private fun analyserLoop(stillLive: () -> Boolean)")
   110	        val copyIn = "synchronized(bandsLock) {\n                        System.arraycopy(bands, 0, publishedBands, 0, SpectrumAnalyzer.BAND_COUNT)\n                    }"
   111	        assertTrue("the analyser copies its picture in under the lock", loop.contains(copyIn))
   112	        assertTrue(
   113	            "and pushes it after the lock closes, never inside it",
   114	            loop.substringAfter(copyIn).trimStart().startsWith("// Outside the lock") &&
   115	                loop.substringAfter(copyIn).substringBefore("LockSupport.parkNanos").contains("pushSpectrum(bands)"),
   116	        )
   117	        assertTrue(
   118	            "a failure zeros the local picture and publishes it both ways before leaving",
   119	            loop.contains("bands.fill(0f)\n            synchronized(bandsLock) { publishedBands.fill(0f) }\n            pushSpectrum(bands)"),
   120	        )
   121	        val push = body(picture, "private fun pushSpectrum(bands: FloatArray)")
   122	        assertTrue("the push reads the slot once", push.contains("val target = listener.get() ?: return"))
   123	        assertTrue("a dead client clears only the listener it was pushing to", push.contains("listener.compareAndSet(target, null)"))
   124	        val captureLoop = body(capture, "private fun captureLoop(active: CaptureSession)")
   125	        assertTrue("the capture thread never touches the listener", !captureLoop.contains("spectrumListener") && !captureLoop.contains("listener"))
   126	        val offer = body(picture, "fun offer(buffer: ByteArray, bytesRead: Int, position: Long)")
   127	        assertTrue("and its offer into the picture takes no lock", !offer.contains("synchronized") && !offer.contains("bandsLock"))
   128	        assertTrue("the getter copies out under the same lock", body(picture, "fun snapshot(): FloatArray").contains("return synchronized(bandsLock) { publishedBands.copyOf() }"))
   129	        assertTrue(
   130	            "and answers a full-length empty picture when no take is open, never an empty array",
   131	            capture.contains("?: return FloatArray(SpectrumAnalyzer.BAND_COUNT)") && capture.contains("return active.picture.snapshot()"),
   132	        )
   133	        assertTrue("the capture thread never takes that lock", !captureLoop.contains("bandsLock"))
   134	    }

   227	    @Test
   228	    fun aPicturePublishedAcrossAStopCannotBringTheRecorderBack() {
   229	        // The picture moves about thirty times a second and Stop can land on any of them. Reading the
   230	        // current state and committing the next one under one lock is what stops a picture prepared
   231	        // before a stop from being committed after it, which would leave the recorder on screen with
   232	        // no take running and nothing left to hide it.
   233	        listOf("fun show()", "fun showNotice(", "fun updateBands(", "fun updateElapsed(", "fun hide()")
   234	            .forEach { mutator ->
   235	                val text = body(overlayState, mutator)
   236	                assertTrue(
   237	                    "$mutator must go through change {}, which reads and commits under one lock",
   238	                    text.contains("change {") || text.contains("change {\n"),
   239	                )
   240	            }
   241	        // Two lines mention `snapshot =`: the field's own declaration, and the single commit inside
   242	        // change {}. A third is a mutator writing the state on its own again.
   243	        val writes = Regex("(?<!var )snapshot = ").findAll(overlayState).count()
   244	        assertTrue("the snapshot must be written in exactly one place, found $writes", writes == 1)
   245	        assertTrue(
   246	            "delivery must read the state it finds, never replay a captured snapshot",
   247	            overlayState.contains("val current = synchronized(lock) {"),
   248	        )
   249	    }

   158	        listOf("preferences.autoStopOnSilence", "preferences.silencePauseSeconds", "preferences.inputDevicePick", "preferences.keepEarbudsReady", "takeId").forEach {
   159	            assertTrue("the start call carries $it", start.substringAfter("startCaptureForTake(").substringBefore(")").contains(it))
   160	        }
   161	        val publish = body(session, "private fun publishLive(")
   162	        assertTrue(publish.contains("synchronized(publishLock)"))
   163	        assertTrue(publish.indexOf("compareAndSet(SessionState.STARTING, SessionState.RECORDING)") < publish.indexOf("surface.show()"))
   164	        val destroy = session.substringAfter("fun destroy()")
   165	        assertTrue("teardown invalidates under the same lock, before cleanup", destroy.indexOf("synchronized(publishLock)") < destroy.indexOf("serviceJob.cancel()"))
   166	        val wait = body(session, "private fun waitForLive()")
   167	        listOf("state.get() != SessionState.STARTING", "pipeline.capture ?: return", "!capturing", "LIVE_WAIT_BOUND_MS").forEach {
   168	            assertTrue("the waiter has its exit: $it", wait.contains(it))
   169	        }
   170	    }
   171	
   172	    @Test
   173	    fun theHistoryDurationIsTheFileLengthReadBeforeTranscriptionCanDeleteIt() {
   174	        val stop = body(session, "private fun stopAndTranscribe()")
   175	        val duration = stop.indexOf("PcmAudio.durationSeconds(File(it).length())")
   176	        assertTrue(duration > 0)
   177	        assertTrue("read after the file is ready", duration > stop.indexOf("waitForFileReady(2_000L)"))
   178	        assertTrue("and before the take is handed to the service", duration < stop.indexOf("finishTakeOrStop()"))
   179	        assertFalse("no wall-clock fallback", stop.contains("System.currentTimeMillis() - recordingStartedAtMs"))
   180	    }
   181	
   182	    @Test
   183	    fun aCancelledTakeStillHandsOverAndFailurePathsStillStop() {
   184	        val cancel = body(session, "private fun cancelCaptureAndFinish(")
   185	        assertTrue(cancel.contains("finishTakeOrStop()"))

   187	        assertTrue(error.contains("pipeline.stopAudioService()"))

     2	
     3	import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
     4	import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
     5	import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
     6	import org.junit.Test
     7	import java.io.File
     8	
     9	/**
    10	 * Drift guards on the capture thread's buffers.
    11	 *
    12	 * These read source text because `AudioCaptureService` is an Android `Service` and cannot be constructed
    13	 * on the JVM, and because the property being protected is "this code does not exist here" rather than a
    14	 * value any API returns. The device run measures the real numbers; this stops the shape regressing.
    15	 */
    16	class CaptureBufferOwnershipTest {
    17	
    18	    private val source =
    19	        File("src/main/java/com/envi/wispr/audio/AudioCaptureService.kt").readText()
    20	    private val detectorFeed =
    21	        File("src/main/java/com/envi/wispr/audio/DetectorFeed.kt").readText()
    22	    private val pcmAudio =
    23	        File("src/main/java/com/envi/wispr/audio/PcmAudio.kt").readText()
    24	
    25	    private fun captureLoopBody(): String {
    26	        val start = source.indexOf("private fun captureLoop(")
    27	        assertTrue("captureLoop must exist", start >= 0)
    28	        val end = source.indexOf("\n    private fun ", start + 1)
    29	        assertTrue("captureLoop must be followed by another declaration", end > start)
    30	        return source.substring(start, end)
    31	    }
    32	
    33	    @Test
    34	    fun captureLoopUsesOnlyPreallocatedBuffers() {
    35	        // The capture thread must do nothing that can make it late: no allocation, no logging on the
    36	        // hot path, no lock another thread can hold. An allocation here is the one that used to live at
    37	        // the top of this loop.
    38	        val body = captureLoopBody()
    39	        assertFalse(
    40	            "captureLoop must not allocate its read buffer; the session owns it",
    41	            body.contains("ByteArray("),
    42	        )
    43	        assertTrue(
    44	            "captureLoop reads the buffer the session preallocated",
    45	            body.contains("active.readBuffer"),
    46	        )
    47	    }
    48	
    49	    @Test
    50	    fun theDetectorBlockIsTwoHundredAndFiftySixMilliseconds() {
    51	        // 4096 samples at 16 kHz, 2 bytes each. The same 256 ms the silence state machine ticks on.
    52	        assertEquals(
    53	            SilenceStopDetectorBlockBytes,
    54	            8_192,
    55	        )
    56	        assertTrue(
    57	            "the detector block is a named constant on the feed, not a literal at the call site (#188)",
    58	            detectorFeed.contains("const val READ_BLOCK_BYTES = 8_192"),
    59	        )
    60	    }
    61	
    62	    @Test
    63	    fun theReadIsThirtyTwoMillisecondsAndIsNotTheDetectorBlock() {
    64	        // 512 samples at 16 kHz, 2 bytes each: short enough that the recorder's picture follows a
    65	        // syllable (#151). The detector still receives whole 256 ms blocks, staged from eight reads,
    66	        // so shrinking the read while keeping the block constant is exactly what must be true here and
    67	        // exactly what the block test above cannot see on its own.
    68	        assertTrue(
    69	            "the read chunk is the audio format's constant, shared by the service and the picture (#188)",
    70	            pcmAudio.contains("const val READ_CHUNK_BYTES = 1_024"),
    71	        )
    72	        assertTrue(
    73	            "the session's read buffer is one chunk",
    74	            source.contains("readBuffer = ByteArray(PcmAudio.READ_CHUNK_BYTES),"),
    75	        )
    76	        assertFalse(
    77	            "and never the detector block",
    78	            source.contains("readBuffer = ByteArray(DetectorFeed.READ_BLOCK_BYTES),"),
    79	        )
    80	        assertTrue(
    81	            "the detector ring and its staging keep the block, inside the feed (#188)",
    82	            detectorFeed.contains("if (autoStop) BlockRing(RING_BLOCKS, READ_BLOCK_BYTES) else null") &&
    83	                detectorFeed.contains("if (autoStop) ByteArray(READ_BLOCK_BYTES) else null"),
    84	        )
    85	    }
    86	
    87	    @Test
    88	    fun theNativeBufferIsSizedSeparatelyFromTheReadBlock() {
    89	        // Two quantities with opposite pressures. Passing the read block to the AudioRecord constructor
    90	        // would shrink the overrun margin; passing the native buffer to read() would restore the
    91	        // one-second granularity this change exists to remove.
    92	        assertTrue(
    93	            "the recorder is constructed with the coerced native size",
    94	            source.contains("ENCODING,\n                    nativeBufferBytes,"),
    95	        )
    96	        assertTrue(
    97	            "the coerced size still floors at one second of audio",
    98	            source.contains("coerceAtLeast(SAMPLE_RATE * PcmAudio.BYTES_PER_SAMPLE)"),
    99	        )
   100	        assertFalse(
   101	            "the read block must not be handed to the AudioRecord constructor",
   102	            source.contains("ENCODING,\n                    READ_BLOCK_BYTES,"),
   103	        )
   104	    }
   105	
   106	    @Test
   107	    fun theRealBufferNumbersAreLoggedRatherThanAssumed() {
   108	        // Whether the one-second floor binds cannot be settled from source, so the phone answers it.
   109	        assertTrue(
   110	            source.contains("Buffer sizes: minimum=\$minimum coerced=\$coerced read=\${PcmAudio.READ_CHUNK_BYTES} block=\${DetectorFeed.READ_BLOCK_BYTES}"),
   111	        )
   112	        assertTrue(
   113	            "and what Android actually allocated, which can exceed what was requested",
   114	            source.contains("record.bufferSizeInFrames"),
   115	        )
   116	    }
   117	
   118	    private companion object {
   119	        /** 4096 samples at 16 kHz, 16-bit mono. Written out rather than computed from the subject. */
   120	        const val SilenceStopDetectorBlockBytes = 4096 * 2
   121	    }
   122	}
     1	package com.envi.wispr.audio
     2	
     3	import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
     4	import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
     5	import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
     6	import org.junit.Test
     7	import java.io.File
     8	
     9	/**
    10	 * Drift Guard (testing-philosophy.md), source text. When this fails, a future edit put an allocation, a
    11	 * log, a lock, a wait or a binder call on the capture thread, which the user feels as dropped audio.
    12	 *
    13	 * Since #188 the capture loop hands every read to two owners through one `offer` each, and those two
    14	 * bodies are the whole of what the limbs may run on the capture thread. The loop itself keeps its own
    15	 * exit logs (the cap, the ceiling, a failure), which `SilenceStopWiringTest` pins, so the forbidden set
    16	 * for the loop is narrower than for the offers.
    17	 */
    18	class CaptureThreadPathTest {
    19	
    20	    private val service = File("src/main/java/com/envi/wispr/audio/AudioCaptureService.kt").readText()
    21	    private val feed = File("src/main/java/com/envi/wispr/audio/DetectorFeed.kt").readText()
    22	    private val picture = File("src/main/java/com/envi/wispr/audio/PicturePublisher.kt").readText()
    23	
    24	    private fun member(source: String, signature: String): String {
    25	        val start = source.indexOf(signature)
    26	        assertTrue("$signature must exist", start >= 0)
    27	        val end = listOf("\n    /**", "\n    private fun ", "\n    fun ", "\n    override fun ")
    28	            .map { source.indexOf(it, start + 1) }.filter { it > start }.minOrNull() ?: source.length
    29	        return source.substring(start, end)
    30	    }
    31	
    32	    @Test
    33	    fun theCaptureThreadOffersAndNeverWaits() {
    34	        val loop = member(service, "private fun captureLoop(active: CaptureSession)")
    35	        assertTrue(loop.contains("active.detector.offer(buffer, bytesRead, position)"))
    36	        assertTrue(loop.contains("active.picture.offer(buffer, bytesRead, position)"))
    37	        val limbCalls = Regex("active\\.(detector|picture)\\.(\\w+)").findAll(loop).map { it.groupValues[2] }.toSet()
    38	        assertEquals("the loop touches the two limbs through offer and nothing else", setOf("offer"), limbCalls)
    39	        listOf("bindService(", "unbindService(", "Thread.sleep", ".transact", "remote.", "ByteArray(").forEach { token ->
    40	            assertFalse("captureLoop must not contain $token", loop.contains(token))
    41	        }
    42	
    43	        val offers = mapOf(
    44	            "DetectorFeed.offer" to member(feed, "fun offer(buffer: ByteArray, bytesRead: Int, position: Long)"),
    45	            "PicturePublisher.offer" to member(picture, "fun offer(buffer: ByteArray, bytesRead: Int, position: Long)"),
    46	        )
    47	        val forbidden = listOf(
    48	            "ByteArray(", "DebugLogger", "synchronized", "Thread.sleep", ".transact", "remote.",
    49	            "bindService(", "unbindService(", "\"",
    50	        )
    51	        offers.forEach { (name, body) ->
    52	            forbidden.forEach { token -> assertFalse("$name must not contain $token", body.contains(token)) }
    53	        }
    54	        assertTrue("the detector offer hands whole blocks to its ring", offers.getValue("DetectorFeed.offer").contains("ring.offer("))
    55	        assertTrue("the picture offer hands the chunk to its ring", offers.getValue("PicturePublisher.offer").contains("spectrumRing.offer("))
    56	    }
    57	}

     9	/**

    39	        assertTrue("release closes the route through closeResources", release.contains("closeResources(active, keepRoute = holding)"))
    40	        assertTrue(member(service, "private fun closeResources(active: CaptureSession, keepRoute: Boolean)").contains("active.route.close(keepRoute)"))
    41	        assertTrue(release.contains("active.detector.close()"))

    46	        assertEquals("the hold is closed twice around the join, both through the owner", 2,

    48	        // The route is the capture thread's: teardown stops the take and joins that thread, whose

    51	        assertFalse("onDestroy leaves the route to the capture thread", destroy.contains("route.close("))

     1	package com.envi.wispr.audio
     2	
     3	import android.media.AudioDeviceCallback
     4	import android.media.AudioDeviceInfo
     5	import android.media.AudioManager
     6	import android.os.Handler
     7	import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
     8	import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
     9	import org.junit.Test
    10	import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicReference
    11	
    12	/**
    13	 * Harness Contract (testing-philosophy.md): the subjects are the four REAL audio owners of #188, and every
    14	 * platform edge each one touches at close is a constructor-injected operation armed to COUNT, so a
    15	 * second `close` that reached a platform object again would read as two, whatever the object itself
    16	 * would have tolerated. When this fails, the user sees the earbuds' route torn down twice, a detector
    17	 * unbound after another take bound it, or a thread interrupted that belongs to the next take.
    18	 *
    19	 * The Android value objects (`AudioDeviceInfo`, `AudioManager`, `Handler`) come from the SDK stubs with
    20	 * `unitTests.isReturnDefaultValues` (app/build.gradle.kts); none of their answers is asserted here.
    21	 */
    22	class AudioLimbCloseTest {
    23	
    24	    /** The route thread's stand-in: remembers what was posted and counts what was removed. */
    25	    private class CountingScheduler : RouteScheduler {
    26	        val posted = ArrayList<Runnable>()
    27	        var removed = 0
    28	        override fun post(runnable: Runnable) { posted += runnable }
    29	        override fun postDelayed(runnable: Runnable, delayMs: Long) { posted += runnable }
    30	        override fun removeCallbacks(runnable: Runnable) { removed++ }
    31	    }
    32	
    33	    private class CountingTrack : WarmHold.SilentTrack {
    34	        var plays = 0
    35	        var stops = 0
    36	        override fun play() { plays++ }
    37	        override fun stop() { stops++ }
    38	    }
    39	
    40	    /** An SDK stub instance: its constructor and methods return defaults, and nothing here reads them. */
    41	    private inline fun <reified T> stub(): T =
    42	        T::class.java.getDeclaredConstructor().also { it.isAccessible = true }.newInstance()
    43	
    44	    private fun bluetoothRoute(hold: RouteHold, scheduler: RouteScheduler, unregister: (AudioDeviceCallback) -> Unit): TakeRoute {
    45	        val info = stub<AudioDeviceInfo>()
    46	        val resolved = ResolvedRoute(
    47	            target = InputDeviceCandidate(id = 7, type = AudioDeviceInfo.TYPE_BLUETOOTH_SCO, name = "Earbuds", isSource = true, isSink = false),
    48	            info = info,
    49	            reason = InputRouteReason.PICKED,
    50	            needsBluetooth = true,
    51	            sink = info,
    52	        )
    53	        return TakeRoute(
    54	            hold = hold,
    55	            resolved = resolved,
    56	            effective = EffectiveDevice(InputRouteReason.PICKED),

    97	    @Test
    98	    fun closingTheRouteTwiceRemovesEachWatchAndReleasesTheHoldOnce() {
    99	        var cleared = 0
   100	        var listenerRemoved = 0
   101	        var unregistered = 0
   102	        val scheduler = CountingScheduler()
   103	        val hold = RouteHold({ cleared++ }, { listenerRemoved++ }).also { it.markCommunicationSet(); it.markListenerSet() }
   104	        val route = bluetoothRoute(hold, scheduler) { unregistered++ }
   105	        route.watchSink(stub<AudioManager>(), Handler())
   106	        route.armDeadline(stub<AudioManager>(), locked = { it() }, stillWaiting = { true }, onRefused = {})
   107	        assertEquals("the deadline was armed", 1, scheduler.posted.size)
   108	        route.close(keepRoute = false)
   109	        route.close(keepRoute = false)
   110	        assertEquals("the deadline is removed once", 1, scheduler.removed)
   111	        assertEquals("the sink watch is unregistered once", 1, unregistered)
   112	        assertEquals("the communication device is cleared once", 1, cleared)
   113	        assertEquals("the routing listener is removed once", 1, listenerRemoved)
   114	        assertTrue(hold.isReleased)
   115	    }

   117	    @Test
   118	    fun closingTheWarmHoldTwiceEndsItOnce() {
   119	        var cleared = 0
   120	        var listenerRemoved = 0
   121	        var commAdded = 0
   122	        var commRemoved = 0
   123	        var registered = 0
   124	        var unregistered = 0
   125	        var kept = 0
   126	        var idle = 0
   127	        val scheduler = CountingScheduler()
   128	        val track = CountingTrack()
   129	        val owner = WarmHoldOwner(
   130	            tag = "test",
   131	            scheduler = scheduler,
   132	            locked = { it() },
   133	            addCommListener = { commAdded++ },
   134	            removeCommListener = { commRemoved++ },
   135	            registerDeviceCallback = { registered++ },
   136	            unregisterDeviceCallback = { unregistered++ },
   137	            newTrack = { track },
   138	            keepAlive = { kept++ },
   139	            onIdle = { idle++ },
   140	        )
   141	        val hold = RouteHold({ cleared++ }, { listenerRemoved++ }).also { it.markCommunicationSet() }
   142	        val route = bluetoothRoute(hold, scheduler) { }
   143	        assertTrue("the hold starts", owner.start(route))
   144	        assertTrue(owner.isActive)
   145	        assertEquals(1, track.plays)
   146	        assertEquals("the expiry was armed", 1, scheduler.posted.size)
   147	        assertEquals(1, commAdded)
   148	        assertEquals(1, registered)
   149	        owner.close(WarmHold.END_DESTROYED)
   150	        owner.close(WarmHold.END_DESTROYED)
   151	        assertTrue(!owner.isActive)
   152	        assertEquals("the silent track stops once", 1, track.stops)
   153	        assertEquals("the expiry is removed once", 1, scheduler.removed)
   154	        assertEquals("the communication listener is removed once", 1, commRemoved)
   155	        assertEquals("the device callback is unregistered once", 1, unregistered)
   156	        assertEquals("the route the hold carried is released once", 1, cleared)
   157	        assertEquals("and the service is told it may stop, once", 1, idle)
   158	        assertEquals("a destroyed hold never asked the service to stay", 0, kept)
   159	    }
   160	}

   439	        synchronized(sessionLock) { if (warmHold?.isActive != true && session == null) stopSelf() }

   443	    private fun startRecording(
   444	        autoStopOnSilence: Boolean,
   445	        pauseSeconds: Float,
   446	        pick: InputDevicePick,
   447	        keepEarbudsReady: Boolean = false,
   448	        takeId: String,
   449	    ): Boolean {
   450	        // REQUESTED is not the same as VALID AND ENABLED. A pause outside the slider's range reaching
   451	        // this binder means a caller we do not control, so the detector is not built at all rather than
   452	        // built with a number nobody chose. Ordinary recording is untouched either way.
   453	        val validPause = pauseSeconds.takeIf {
   454	            it.isFinite() &&
   455	                it >= SilenceStopDetector.MIN_PAUSE_SECONDS &&
   456	                it <= SilenceStopDetector.MAX_PAUSE_SECONDS
   457	        }
   458	        val detectorEnabled = autoStopOnSilence && validPause != null
   459	        val requestedButRefused = autoStopOnSilence && validPause == null
   460	
   461	        synchronized(sessionLock) {
   462	            // A stopped session remains here until its reader has closed both resources.
   463	            // Starting another take before that point would make the old thread write into
   464	            // the new take's file or release the new take's AudioRecord.
   465	            if (session != null) return false
   466	            lastStartFailure = START_FAILURE_NONE
   467	
   468	            // Route ownership exists BEFORE the session, so every failure path below can release it.
   469	            val audioManager = getSystemService(AUDIO_SERVICE) as AudioManager
   470	            // Filled in once the listener exists; the hold removes exactly this one and no other.
   471	            var routingListener: Pair<AudioRecord, AudioRouting.OnRoutingChangedListener>? = null
   472	            val routeHold = RouteHold(
   473	                clearCommunicationDevice = { audioManager.clearCommunicationDevice() },
   474	                removeListener = { routingListener?.let { (r, l) -> r.removeOnRoutingChangedListener(l) } },
   475	            )
   476	            // A warm hold hands its route to this take (the link stays up; V13: live at ~120 ms). Any hold
   477	            // that does not match the resolved target is ended by resolveRoute before it sets anything.
   478	            // The identity is read BEFORE the handover: handOver ends the hold, and the hold's end clears
   479	            // its bookkeeping synchronously (Codex review 1).
   480	            val handedOver = warmHold?.let { hold ->
   481	                val type = heldSinkType
   482	                val name = heldSinkName
   483	                hold.handOver()?.let { HandedRoute(it, type, name) }
   484	            }
   485	            val route = resolveRoute(audioManager, pick, routeHold, handedOver) ?: run {
   486	                lastStartFailure = START_FAILURE_NO_INPUT_DEVICE
   487	                routeHold.release()
   488	                DebugLogger.error(TAG, "No input device at all; refusing to start")
   489	                stopSelf()
   490	                return false
   491	            }
   492	
   493	            val nativeBufferBytes = try {
   494	                val minimum = AudioRecord.getMinBufferSize(SAMPLE_RATE, CHANNEL, ENCODING)
   495	                    .takeIf { it > 0 }
   496	                    ?: throw IllegalStateException("AudioRecord buffer size unavailable")
   497	                val coerced = minimum.coerceAtLeast(SAMPLE_RATE * PcmAudio.BYTES_PER_SAMPLE)
   498	                // Whether the floor binds cannot be settled from source: getMinBufferSize is computed by
   499	                // the platform from the device's own frame count. Log both so the answer comes from the
   500	                // phone rather than from an assumption. Android may also enlarge what it actually
   501	                // allocates, which getBufferSizeInFrames reports once the recorder exists.
   502	                DebugLogger.log(
   503	                    TAG,
   504	                    "Buffer sizes: minimum=$minimum coerced=$coerced read=$READ_CHUNK_BYTES block=$READ_BLOCK_BYTES",
   505	                )
   506	                coerced
   507	            } catch (e: Exception) {
   508	                DebugLogger.error(TAG, "Failed to determine audio buffer size", e)
   509	                lastStartFailure = START_FAILURE_OTHER
   510	                routeHold.release()
   511	                stopSelf()
   512	                return false
   513	            }
   514	
   515	            var record: AudioRecord? = null
   516	            var output: FileOutputStream? = null
   517	            try {
   518	                record = AudioRecord(
   519	                    MediaRecorder.AudioSource.VOICE_RECOGNITION,
   520	                    SAMPLE_RATE,
   521	                    CHANNEL,
   522	                    ENCODING,
   523	                    nativeBufferBytes,
   524	                )
   525	                if (record.state != AudioRecord.STATE_INITIALIZED) {
   526	                    throw IllegalStateException("AudioRecord failed to initialize")
   527	                }
