   581	                DebugLogger.log(
   582	                    TAG,
   583	                    "route start=${effective.label()} kind=${effective.kind} reason=${route.reason} " +
   584	                        "target=${route.target.label}",
   585	                )
   586	
   587	                if (requestedButRefused) {
   588	                    // The caller asked for auto-stop and cannot have it, which is exactly the state the
   589	                    // notice exists for. Recording itself is unaffected.
   590	                    newSession.silenceStatus.set(SILENCE_STATUS_UNAVAILABLE)
   591	                    DebugLogger.warn(TAG, "Auto-stop refused: pause $pauseSeconds is out of range")
   592	                }
   593	                if (detectorEnabled) startSilenceDetection(newSession, validPause!!)
   594	
   595	                val thread = Thread({ captureLoop(newSession) }, "AudioCaptureThread")
   596	                captureThread = thread
   597	                try {
   598	                    thread.start()
   599	                } catch (e: Exception) {
   600	                    isRecording.set(false)
   601	                    session = null
   602	                    closeResources(newSession, keepRoute = false)
   603	                    captureThread = null
   604	                    stopSelf()
   605	                    DebugLogger.error(TAG, "Failed to start capture thread", e)
   606	                    return false
   607	                }
   608	                startSpectrumAnalysis(newSession)
   609	                if (route.needsBluetooth) {
   610	                    watchSink(newSession)
   611	                    armDeadline(newSession)
   612	                } else {
   613	                    markLive(newSession)
   614	                }
   615	                return thread.isAlive && session === newSession && isRecording.get()
   616	            } catch (e: SecurityException) {
   617	                DebugLogger.error(TAG, "RECORD_AUDIO permission not granted", e)
   618	                lastStartFailure = START_FAILURE_OTHER
   619	                isRecording.set(false)
   620	                routeHold.release()
   621	                closeResources(record, output)
   622	                stopSelf()
   623	                return false
   624	            } catch (e: Exception) {
   625	                DebugLogger.error(TAG, "Failed to start recording", e)
   626	                lastStartFailure = START_FAILURE_OTHER
   627	                isRecording.set(false)
   628	                routeHold.release()
   629	                closeResources(record, output)
   630	                stopSelf()
   631	                return false
   632	            }
   633	        }
   634	    }
   659	    private fun resolveRoute(
   660	        audioManager: AudioManager,
   661	        pick: InputDevicePick,
   662	        hold: RouteHold,
   663	        handedOver: HandedRoute?,
   664	    ): ResolvedRoute? {
   665	        val infos = audioManager.getDevices(AudioManager.GET_DEVICES_INPUTS).toList()
   666	        val candidates = infos.map(InputDeviceCandidate::from)
   667	        val resolution = InputDeviceResolver.resolve(pick, candidates)
   668	        val target = resolution.target ?: run { handedOver?.route?.release(); return null }
   669	        var reason = resolution.reason
   670	        var sinkInfo: AudioDeviceInfo? = null
   671	
   672	        if (InputDeviceResolver.needsBluetoothRoute(target)) {
   673	            val opened = runCatching {
   674	                val available = audioManager.availableCommunicationDevices
   675	                val sink = InputDeviceResolver.communicationSinkFor(target, available.map(InputDeviceCandidate::from))
   676	                    ?: return@runCatching false
   677	                sinkInfo = available.first { it.id == sink.id }
   678	                val held = handedOver != null && handedOver.sinkType == sink.type && handedOver.sinkName == sink.name
   679	                if (held) {
   680	                    hold.adoptCommunicationFrom(handedOver!!.route)
   681	                    DebugLogger.log(TAG, "route adopt=${target.label} from the warm hold")
   682	                    true
   683	                } else {
   684	                    handedOver?.route?.release()
   685	                    audioManager.setCommunicationDevice(sinkInfo!!).also { if (it) hold.markCommunicationSet() }
   686	                }
   687	            }.getOrElse { e ->
   688	                DebugLogger.warn(TAG, "setCommunicationDevice threw: ${e.message}")
   689	                false
   690	            }
   691	            if (!opened) {
   692	                DebugLogger.warn(TAG, "Bluetooth link refused for ${target.label}; staying on the earbuds")
   693	                handedOver?.route?.release()
   694	                hold.releaseCommunicationDevice()
   695	                reason = InputRouteReason.LINK_REFUSED
   696	            }
   697	        } else {
   698	            handedOver?.route?.release()
   699	        }
   700	        val info = infos.firstOrNull { it.id == target.id } ?: return null
   701	        return ResolvedRoute(
   702	            target = target,
   703	            info = info,
   704	            reason = reason,
   705	            needsBluetooth = InputDeviceResolver.needsBluetoothRoute(target),
   706	            sink = sinkInfo,
   707	        )
   708	    }
   709	
   710	    /**
   711	     * Only a Bluetooth target, or any explicit pick, names a preferred device; Auto on wired, USB or the
   712	     * phone leaves today's behaviour untouched. A refusal is recorded and the take proceeds on whatever
   713	     * Android routes, reported truthfully by `routedDevice`, never by the target.
   714	     */
   715	    private fun applyPreferredDevice(record: AudioRecord, route: ResolvedRoute, effective: EffectiveDevice, hold: RouteHold) {
   716	        if (!route.needsBluetooth && route.reason != InputRouteReason.PICKED) return
   717	        val accepted = runCatching { record.setPreferredDevice(route.info) }.getOrDefault(false)
   718	        if (!accepted) {
   719	            DebugLogger.warn(TAG, "setPreferredDevice refused for ${route.target.label}")
   720	            effective.markReason(InputRouteReason.PREFERRED_REFUSED)
   721	            // The link is given back; listener ownership stays with the take so its route changes are recorded.
   722	            hold.releaseCommunicationDevice()
   723	        }
   724	    }
   725	
   726	    /**
   727	     * Registered on the route thread, removed by the hold. A callback checks the hold before writing so
   728	     * one already running when cleanup starts writes nothing; a callback for a dead session finds its
   729	     * own session object, never the live one.
   730	     */
   731	    private fun registerRoutingListener(record: AudioRecord, active: CaptureSession): AudioRouting.OnRoutingChangedListener? {
   732	        val listener = AudioRouting.OnRoutingChangedListener { router ->
   733	            if (active.routeHold.isReleased) return@OnRoutingChangedListener
   734	            val device = runCatching { router.routedDevice }.getOrNull() ?: return@OnRoutingChangedListener
   735	            active.effective.observe(device.type, device.productName?.toString().orEmpty())
   736	            DebugLogger.log(TAG, "route change=${active.effective.label()} at ${active.bytesWritten} bytes")
   737	        }
   738	        return runCatching {
   739	            record.addOnRoutingChangedListener(listener, routeHandler)
   740	        }.map {
   741	            active.routeHold.markListenerSet()
   742	            listener
   743	        }.onFailure { DebugLogger.warn(TAG, "Routing listener not registered: ${it.message}") }
   744	            .getOrNull()
   745	    }
   746	
   747	    /** Read the final route while the recorder is still active. Null preserves the history as it stands. */
   748	    private fun observeFinalRoute(active: CaptureSession) {
   749	        val device = runCatching { active.record.routedDevice }.getOrNull() ?: return
   750	        active.effective.observe(device.type, device.productName?.toString().orEmpty())
   751	    }
   752	
   753	    /**
   754	     * May a read on the OBSERVED route open the gate? An earbud target that Android is routing to the
   755	     * phone may not, unless the phone was picked or the earbuds have left: the founder's rule, applied
   756	     * to the observation and never to the request. A route not yet observed is not refused.
   757	     *
   758	     * The rule binds what this app SELECTS, and is enforced at the gate. A route Android moves by itself
   759	     * once the take is live (V7, a call taking the link) is recorded on the History card ("AirPods Pro 3,
   760	     * then Phone") and not fought: ending a take mid-sentence would lose the words, and capture must never
   761	     * fail (architecture: heart and limbs). Decided at Codex code review 5, 2026-09-18.
   762	     */
   763	    private fun routeAdmissible(active: CaptureSession): Boolean =
   764	        !active.targetBluetooth || active.phonePicked || active.sinkGone ||
   765	            active.effective.currentKind != InputRouteKind.PHONE
   766	
   767	    /**
   768	     * Watch the take's earbuds leave, on the route thread, so the gate can admit the phone once they
   769	     * are gone (V7: Android moves the route itself within 120 ms). Registered after the session exists,
   770	     * removed in [releaseSession].
   771	     */
   772	    private fun watchSink(active: CaptureSession) {
   773	        val sink = active.sink ?: return
   774	        val type = sink.type
   775	        val name = sink.productName?.toString().orEmpty()
   776	        val audioManager = getSystemService(AUDIO_SERVICE) as AudioManager
   777	        val callback = object : AudioDeviceCallback() {
   778	            override fun onAudioDevicesRemoved(removed: Array<out AudioDeviceInfo>) {
   779	                if (removed.any { it.type == type && it.productName?.toString().orEmpty() == name }) {
   780	                    active.sinkGone = true
   781	                    DebugLogger.log(TAG, "route earbuds removed while ${active.gate.state}; the phone may record")
   782	                }
   783	            }
   784	        }
   785	        active.sinkWatch = callback
   786	        runCatching { audioManager.registerAudioDeviceCallback(callback, routeHandler) }
   787	            .onFailure { DebugLogger.warn(TAG, "sink watch not registered: ${it.message}") }
   788	        // Reconcile once: a removal between route resolution and this registration is not replayed by
   789	        // the callback (Codex review 5). The list is read AFTER registering, so nothing can fall between.
   790	        val stillOffered = runCatching {
   791	            audioManager.availableCommunicationDevices.any { it.type == type && it.productName?.toString().orEmpty() == name }
   792	        }.getOrDefault(true)
   793	        if (!stillOffered) {
   794	            active.sinkGone = true
   795	            DebugLogger.log(TAG, "route earbuds already gone at start; the phone may record")
   796	        }
   797	    }
   798	
   799	    /** Capture thread, on the read that opened the gate. */
   800	    private fun markLive(active: CaptureSession) {
   801	        active.liveAtMs = SystemClock.elapsedRealtime()
   802	        routeHandler.post {
   803	            active.deadline?.let { routeHandler.removeCallbacks(it) }
   804	            active.deadline = null
   805	            DebugLogger.log(
   806	                TAG,
   807	                "route live=${active.effective.label()} after ${active.liveAtMs - active.startedAtMs} ms " +
   808	                    "resets=${active.gate.resetsUsed} state=${active.gate.state}",
   809	            )
   810	        }
   811	    }
   812	
   813	    /**
   814	     * The live deadline runs on the route thread as a clock, so a blocked read cannot starve it. First
   815	     * miss: reset the communication device once, if the sink is still there. Second miss: proceed without
   816	     * sound on the earbuds (FORCED), or, when the observed route is the phone with earbuds connected,
   817	     * fail the take rather than record from the phone.
   818	     */
   819	    private fun armDeadline(active: CaptureSession) {
   820	        val runnable = object : Runnable {
   821	            override fun run() {
   822	                synchronized(sessionLock) {
   823	                    if (session !== active || !isRecording.get() || active.gate.state != LiveGate.State.WAITING) return
   824	                    when (active.gate.deadlinePassed()) {
   825	                        LiveGate.DeadlineAction.NONE -> return
   826	                        LiveGate.DeadlineAction.RESET -> {
   827	                            val reset = resetCommunicationDevice(active)
   828	                            DebugLogger.warn(TAG, "route reset=${active.effective.label()} performed=$reset after ${LiveGate.DEADLINE_MS} ms")
   829	                            active.deadline = this
   830	                            routeHandler.postDelayed(this, LiveGate.DEADLINE_MS)
   831	                        }
   832	                        LiveGate.DeadlineAction.FORCE -> {
   833	                            active.deadline = null
   834	                            if (routeAdmissible(active)) {
   835	                                active.gate.force()
   836	                                active.liveAtMs = SystemClock.elapsedRealtime()
   837	                                DebugLogger.warn(TAG, "route forced=${active.effective.label()} after ${active.liveAtMs - active.startedAtMs} ms")
   838	                            } else {
   839	                                lastStartFailure = START_FAILURE_EARBUDS
   840	                                DebugLogger.warn(TAG, "route refused=${active.effective.label()}: earbuds connected, phone would record; failing the take")
   841	                                endTakeLocked(active, TERMINAL_REASON_ERROR)
   842	                            }
   843	                        }
   844	                    }
   845	                }
   846	            }
   847	        }
   848	        active.deadline = runnable
   849	        routeHandler.postDelayed(runnable, LiveGate.DEADLINE_MS)
   850	    }
   851	
   852	    /** Route thread, under `sessionLock`. Clear and re-select the sink, only while it is still offered. */
   853	    private fun resetCommunicationDevice(active: CaptureSession): Boolean {
   854	        val sink = active.sink ?: return false
   855	        val audioManager = getSystemService(AUDIO_SERVICE) as AudioManager
   856	        val stillThere = runCatching {
   857	            audioManager.availableCommunicationDevices.any { it.type == sink.type && it.productName?.toString() == sink.productName?.toString() }
   858	        }.getOrDefault(false)
   859	        if (!stillThere) return false
   860	        return runCatching {
   861	            audioManager.clearCommunicationDevice()
   862	            audioManager.setCommunicationDevice(sink).also { if (it) active.routeHold.markCommunicationSet() }
   863	        }.getOrElse { e ->
   864	            DebugLogger.warn(TAG, "communication device reset threw: ${e.message}")
   865	            false
   866	        }
   867	    }
   869	    private fun captureLoop(active: CaptureSession) {
   870	        val buffer = active.readBuffer
   871	        try {
   872	            while (isRecording.get() && session === active) {
   873	                // The cap counts from LIVE; the wait for the earbuds has its own bound in the session owner.
   874	                val live = active.liveAtMs
   875	                val elapsed = if (live > 0L) SystemClock.elapsedRealtime() - live else 0L
   876	                if (elapsed >= RecordingLimits.MAX_DURATION_MS) {
   877	                    // The reason is the whole signal. The session owner reads it back through
   878	                    // getTerminalReason and is what tells the user why their take ended.
   879	                    claimEnding(active, TERMINAL_REASON_MAX_DURATION)
   880	                    DebugLogger.log(TAG, "Max duration reached (${elapsed}ms), auto-stopping")
   881	                    break
   882	                }
   883	
   884	                // THE CLOCK CANNOT BOUND THE FILE, because the two measure different things.
   885	                // `record.startRecording()` runs before `startedAtMs` is taken, so the hardware is
   886	                // already buffering when the clock starts, and the check above can pass on its last
   887	                // pass with the file already at the ceiling. One more whole block then goes in.
   888	                //
   889	                // That is not a rounding error. `AsrService` REFUSES a file over the ceiling rather
   890	                // than truncating it, so a few bytes past it discards the entire take: exactly the
   891	                // long dictation this limit exists to keep. So the read is bounded by what is left.
   892	                val remaining = RecordingLimits.MAX_AUDIO_BYTES - active.bytesWritten
   893	                if (remaining <= 0L) {
   894	                    claimEnding(active, TERMINAL_REASON_MAX_DURATION)
   895	                    DebugLogger.log(TAG, "Byte ceiling reached (${active.bytesWritten} bytes), auto-stopping")
   896	                    break
   897	                }
   898	
   899	                // Explicit, because the three-argument overload's blocking behaviour is a default
   900	                // rather than a statement, and this loop's timing depends on it.
   901	                val requested = minOf(buffer.size.toLong(), remaining).toInt()
   902	                val bytesRead = active.record.read(buffer, 0, requested, AudioRecord.READ_BLOCKING)
   903	                if (bytesRead < 0) throw IOException("AudioRecord.read failed: $bytesRead")
   904	                if (bytesRead == 0) continue
   905	
   906	                // Until the gate opens, a read feeds the gate and nothing else: not the file, not the
   907	                // detector, not the picture, not the level. The take's clock starts when the gate opens.
   908	                if (active.gate.state == LiveGate.State.WAITING) {
   909	                    val admissible = routeAdmissible(active)
   910	                    if (active.gate.offer(buffer, bytesRead, admissible)) markLive(active) else continue
   911	                }
   912	
   913	                val position = active.bytesWritten
   914	                active.output.write(buffer, 0, bytesRead)
   915	                active.bytesWritten += bytesRead
   916	                active.liveVisible = true
   917	                offerToDetector(active, buffer, bytesRead, position)
   918	                // The picture is a limb: a refused offer drops this chunk and nothing else. The analyser
   919	                // sees the drop as a jump in position and starts its window afresh (SpectrumAnalyzer).
   920	                active.spectrumRing.offer(buffer, bytesRead, position)
   921	                LockSupport.unpark(active.analyserThread)
   922	
   923	                var sum = 0L
   924	                var peak = 0
   925	                for (i in 0 until bytesRead step PcmAudio.BYTES_PER_SAMPLE) {
   926	                    if (i + 1 < bytesRead) {
   927	                        val sample = (buffer[i].toInt() and 0xFF) or
   928	                            (buffer[i + 1].toInt() shl 8)
   929	                        val magnitude = abs(sample)
   930	                        sum += magnitude
   931	                        if (magnitude > peak) peak = magnitude
   932	                    }
   933	                }
   934	                val numSamples = bytesRead / PcmAudio.BYTES_PER_SAMPLE
   935	                currentAmplitude = if (numSamples > 0) {
   936	                    (sum.toFloat() / numSamples) / Short.MAX_VALUE
   937	                } else 0f
   938	                // No allocation, one compare per read: the take's peak, kept for the reader at stop.
   939	                val peakLevel = peak.toFloat() / Short.MAX_VALUE
   940	                if (peakLevel > takePeakAmplitude) takePeakAmplitude = peakLevel
   941	            }
   942	        } catch (e: Exception) {
   943	            synchronized(sessionLock) {
   944	                if (session === active) claimEnding(active, TERMINAL_REASON_ERROR)
   945	            }
   946	            DebugLogger.error(TAG, "Capture thread error", e)
   947	        } finally {
   948	            releaseSession(active)
   949	        }
   950	    }
   951	
   952	    /**
   953	     * Capture thread only. Copies audio toward the detector and never waits for it.
   954	     *
   955	     * A read that does not land on a block boundary is staged, so the detector always sees whole 256 ms
   956	     * blocks in order. **Nothing here logs, allocates, locks or calls across a process.**
   957	     *
   958	     * A full ring means the detector has fallen further behind than it can recover from. Auto-stop is
   959	     * abandoned for the rest of the take and never resumed: resuming across dropped audio breaks the
   960	     * model's recurrent continuity, and speech that resumed inside the gap could then read as silence.
   961	     */
   962	    private fun offerToDetector(active: CaptureSession, buffer: ByteArray, bytesRead: Int, position: Long) {
   963	        val ring = active.ring ?: return
   964	        val pending = active.pendingBlock ?: return
   965	        if (active.detectorAbandoned.get()) return
   966	
   967	        var consumed = 0
   968	        while (consumed < bytesRead) {
   969	            if (active.pendingBytes == 0) active.pendingPosition = position + consumed
   970	            val room = READ_BLOCK_BYTES - active.pendingBytes
   971	            val take = minOf(room, bytesRead - consumed)
   972	            System.arraycopy(buffer, consumed, pending, active.pendingBytes, take)
   973	            active.pendingBytes += take
   974	            consumed += take
   975	            if (active.pendingBytes == READ_BLOCK_BYTES) {
   976	                active.pendingBytes = 0
   977	                if (!ring.offer(pending, READ_BLOCK_BYTES, active.pendingPosition)) {
   978	                    // Flag only. The feeder notices and does the logging, off this thread.
   979	                    abandonDetector(active)
   980	                    return
   981	                }
   982	            }
   983	        }
   984	    }
   993	    private fun startSpectrumAnalysis(active: CaptureSession) {
   994	        runCatching {
   995	            val thread = Thread({ analyserLoop(active) }, "SpectrumAnalyserThread")
   996	            active.analyserThread = thread
   997	            thread.start()
   998	        }.onFailure {
   999	            active.analyserThread = null
  1000	            DebugLogger.warn(TAG, "Live picture unavailable for this take: ${it.message}")
  1001	        }
  1002	    }
  1003	
  1004	    /**
  1005	     * Analyser thread only. Drains the picture ring, analyses every queued chunk in order and publishes
  1006	     * once per wake, so what the recorder reads is always the newest audio the ring held.
  1007	     *
  1008	     * Exits when its take is no longer the live one, when its ending has been claimed, or when it is
  1009	     * interrupted, and checks all three at least every [ANALYSER_PARK_NS] whether or not the capture
  1010	     * thread unparks it. Never joined: it holds nothing a stop waits for.
  1011	     *
  1012	     * A failure publishes the zero picture before leaving, so the rail rests rather than holding the
  1013	     * last shape it was given, and it costs the picture only: the take does not know this thread exists.
  1014	     */
  1015	    private fun analyserLoop(active: CaptureSession) {
  1016	        val chunk = ByteArray(READ_CHUNK_BYTES)
  1017	        val bands = FloatArray(SpectrumAnalyzer.BAND_COUNT)
  1018	        try {
  1019	            val analyzer = SpectrumAnalyzer()
  1020	            while (session === active && !active.stopRequested && !Thread.currentThread().isInterrupted) {
  1021	                var analysed = false
  1022	                while (true) {
  1023	                    val length = active.spectrumRing.poll(chunk)
  1024	                    if (length < 0) break
  1025	                    analyzer.analyze(chunk, length, active.spectrumRing.lastPolledTag, bands)
  1026	                    analysed = true
  1027	                }
  1028	                if (analysed) {
  1029	                    synchronized(active.bandsLock) {
  1030	                        System.arraycopy(bands, 0, active.publishedBands, 0, SpectrumAnalyzer.BAND_COUNT)
  1031	                    }
  1032	                    // Outside the lock: the push is a binder transaction and the lock is the getter's.
  1033	                    pushSpectrum(active, bands)
  1034	                }
  1035	                LockSupport.parkNanos(ANALYSER_PARK_NS)
  1036	            }
  1037	        } catch (e: Exception) {
  1038	            bands.fill(0f)
  1039	            synchronized(active.bandsLock) { active.publishedBands.fill(0f) }
  1040	            pushSpectrum(active, bands)
  1041	            DebugLogger.warn(TAG, "Live picture stopped for this take: ${e.message}")
  1042	        } finally {
  1043	            if (active.analyserThread === Thread.currentThread()) active.analyserThread = null
  1044	        }
  1045	    }
  1046	
  1047	    /**
  1048	     * Analyser thread only. Hand one picture to the registered listener, if any (#187).
  1049	     *
  1050	     * `oneway`, so this never waits on the app process; the parcel is written before the call returns,
  1051	     * so the analyser's own array is safe to pass. A dead client throws: the slot is cleared with
  1052	     * `compareAndSet` so a registration that replaced this one in the meantime is kept, and the loss is
  1053	     * logged once per take. The picture is a limb: nothing here can reach the capture thread or the take.
  1054	     */
  1055	    private fun pushSpectrum(active: CaptureSession, bands: FloatArray) {
  1056	        val listener = spectrumListener.get() ?: return
  1057	        try {
  1058	            listener.onSpectrum(bands)
  1059	            active.spectrumPushes.incrementAndGet()
  1060	        } catch (e: RemoteException) {
  1061	            if (spectrumListener.compareAndSet(listener, null)) {
  1062	                DebugLogger.warn(TAG, "Live picture listener gone: ${e.javaClass.simpleName}")
  1063	            }
  1064	        }
  1065	    }
  1066	
  1067	    /**
  1068	     * Bind the detector process for one take and start the one thread allowed to talk to it.
  1069	     *
  1070	     * Capture has already started by the time this runs, so a slow or failed detector delays nothing. A
  1071	     * take whose detector never becomes ready is simply a take the user stops by hand.
  1072	     */
  1073	    private fun startSilenceDetection(active: CaptureSession, pauseSeconds: Float) {
  1074	        val connection = vadConnectionFor(active)
  1075	        active.vadConnection = connection
  1076	
  1077	        active.vadBound = runCatching {
  1078	            bindService(
  1079	                Intent(this, SilenceVadService::class.java),
  1080	                connection,
  1081	                Context.BIND_AUTO_CREATE,
  1082	            )
  1083	        }.getOrDefault(false)
  1084	
  1085	        if (!active.vadBound) {
  1086	            active.vadConnection = null
  1087	            abandonDetector(active)
  1088	            DebugLogger.warn(TAG, "Auto-stop unavailable: detector service could not be bound")
  1089	            return
  1090	        }
  1091	
  1092	        val thread = Thread({ feederLoop(active, pauseSeconds) }, "SilenceFeederThread")
  1093	        active.feederThread = thread
  1094	        runCatching { thread.start() }
  1095	            .onFailure {
  1096	                active.feederThread = null

  1103	    /**
  1104	     * The only thread that calls the detector. It is allowed to block; the capture thread is not.
  1105	     *
  1106	     * The abandonment flag is checked at the top of every pass AND immediately after every remote call,
  1107	     * because a gap can open while a call is in flight and a verdict computed from the blocks before a
  1108	     * gap must never be applied to the audio after it.
  1109	     */
  1110	    private fun feederLoop(active: CaptureSession, pauseSeconds: Float) {
  1111	        val ring = active.ring ?: return
  1112	        val block = ByteArray(READ_BLOCK_BYTES)
  1113	        var started = false
  1114	        var reportedAbandon = false
  1115	
  1116	        fun shouldStop(): Boolean {

  1123	            }

  1127	        try {
  1128	            while (!shouldStop()) {
  1129	                val remote = active.vadService
  1130	                if (remote == null) {

  1132	                    continue
  1133	                }

  1135	                if (!started) {

  1137	                    // id) keeps the old transaction (issue #176).
  1138	                    val status = runCatching {
  1139	                        if (active.takeId.isEmpty()) remote.start(active.token, pauseSeconds)

  1141	                    }
  1142	                        .getOrElse {
  1143	                            abandonDetector(active)

  1145	                            return
  1146	                        }

  1148	                    if (status != SilenceVadService.STATUS_READY) {
  1149	                        abandonDetector(active)
  1150	                        DebugLogger.warn(TAG, "Auto-stop unavailable: the detector reported so")
  1151	                        return
  1152	                    }
  1153	                    started = true
  1154	                    active.silenceStatus.compareAndSet(
  1155	                        SILENCE_STATUS_PREPARING,
  1156	                        SILENCE_STATUS_READY,
  1157	                    )
  1158	                }
  1159	
  1160	                val length = ring.poll(block)
  1161	                if (length <= 0) {
  1162	                    Thread.sleep(FEEDER_IDLE_MS)
  1163	                    continue
  1164	                }
  1165	
  1166	                val result = runCatching { remote.processBlock(active.token, block) }
  1167	                    .getOrElse {
  1168	                        abandonDetector(active)
  1169	                        DebugLogger.warn(TAG, "Auto-stop unavailable: the detector call failed")
  1170	                        return
  1171	                    }
  1172	
  1173	                if (shouldStop()) return
  1174	
  1175	                when (result) {
  1176	                    SilenceVadService.RESULT_SILENCE -> {
  1177	                        // endTake re-checks that this session is still the live one, under the lock, so
  1178	                        // a verdict from a finished take cannot end the take running now.
  1179	                        endTake(active, TERMINAL_REASON_SILENCE)

  1181	                    }
  1182	
  1183	                    SilenceVadService.RESULT_UNAVAILABLE -> {
  1184	                        abandonDetector(active)
  1185	                        DebugLogger.warn(TAG, "Auto-stop unavailable: the detector gave up mid-take")
  1186	                        return
  1187	                    }
  1188	                }
  1189	            }
  1190	        } catch (interrupted: InterruptedException) {
  1191	            Thread.currentThread().interrupt()
  1192	        } catch (e: Exception) {
  1193	            abandonDetector(active)
  1194	            DebugLogger.warn(TAG, "Auto-stop unavailable: the feeder failed, ${e.message}")
  1195	        } finally {
  1196	            if (started) runCatching { active.vadService?.finish(active.token) }
  1197	            unbindVad(active)

  1200	    }

  1202	    /**
  1203	     * Auto-stop is off for the rest of THIS take, and is never resumed within it.
  1204	     *
  1205	     * The status it lands on records whether the detector ever worked. A take that never got one tells
  1206	     * the user; a take that had one and lost it does not, because that recording is still correct and a
  1207	     * message part way through is an interruption for nothing.

  1209	    private fun abandonDetector(active: CaptureSession) {
  1210	        active.detectorAbandoned.set(true)
  1211	        while (true) {
  1212	            val previous = active.silenceStatus.get()
  1213	            val next = when (previous) {
  1214	                SILENCE_STATUS_DISABLED,
  1215	                SILENCE_STATUS_UNAVAILABLE,
  1216	                SILENCE_STATUS_LOST_AFTER_READY -> return
  1217	
  1218	                SILENCE_STATUS_READY -> SILENCE_STATUS_LOST_AFTER_READY
  1219	                else -> SILENCE_STATUS_UNAVAILABLE
  1220	            }
  1221	            if (active.silenceStatus.compareAndSet(previous, next)) return
  1222	        }
  1223	    }

  1225	    /** Unbinds only [active]'s own connection, so a finished take cannot unbind a running one's. */

  1234	    }

  1250	    /** The user, or anything else with the authority to end whatever take is running. */

  1259	    /**

  1273	    /**

  1304	            TAG,
  1305	            "Stopped by ${active.endingClaim.ending.label}. ${active.bytesWritten} bytes " +
  1306	                "(${String.format("%.1f", PcmAudio.durationSeconds(active.bytesWritten))}s) -> ${active.file.absolutePath}",
  1307	        )

  1310	    private fun releaseSession(active: CaptureSession) {
  1311	        var holding = false
  1312	        synchronized(sessionLock) {
  1313	            if (session !== active) return
  1314	            active.deadline?.let { routeHandler.removeCallbacks(it) }
  1315	            active.deadline = null
  1316	            active.sinkWatch?.let { w ->
  1317	                runCatching { (getSystemService(AUDIO_SERVICE) as AudioManager).unregisterAudioDeviceCallback(w) }
  1318	            }

  1321	            observeFinalRoute(active)

  1323	            // may keep the earbuds warm; an error ending and teardown release everything.

  1344	        if (!holding) stopSelf()

  1347	    /**

  1360	    // ---- The warm hold ----
  1361	
  1362	    /** Under `sessionLock`. */
  1363	    private fun holdEligible(active: CaptureSession): Boolean {
  1364	        if (destroyed) return false
  1365	        if (!active.keepEarbudsReady || !active.targetBluetooth || active.sink == null) return false
  1366	        if (active.routeHold.isReleased) return false
  1367	        if (active.effective.currentKind != InputRouteKind.BLUETOOTH) return false
  1368	        // Exhaustive, no else: a new ending decides here whether it keeps the earbuds warm.
  1369	        return when (active.endingClaim.ending) {
  1370	            CaptureEnding.Manual, CaptureEnding.Silence, CaptureEnding.MaxDuration -> true
  1371	            CaptureEnding.StillRunning, CaptureEnding.Failure -> false
  1372	        }
  1373	    }
  1374	
  1375	    /** Under `sessionLock`. True when the hold is playing and now owns the route. */
  1376	    private fun startWarmHold(active: CaptureSession): Boolean {
  1377	        val sink = active.sink ?: return false
  1378	        val audioManager = getSystemService(AUDIO_SERVICE) as AudioManager
  1379	        val label = active.effective.label()
  1380	        val hold = WarmHold(
  1381	            route = active.routeHold,
  1382	            track = AudioTrackSilence(),
  1383	            onEnded = { reason -> onHoldEnded(reason, label) },
  1384	        )
  1385	        heldSinkType = sink.type
  1386	        heldSinkName = sink.productName?.toString().orEmpty()
  1387	        warmHold = hold
  1388	        if (!hold.start()) {
  1389	            clearHoldBookkeeping()
  1390	            return false
  1391	        }
  1392	        val expiry = Runnable { synchronized(sessionLock) { if (warmHold === hold) hold.end(WarmHold.END_EXPIRED) } }
  1393	        holdExpiry = expiry
  1394	        routeHandler.postDelayed(expiry, WarmHold.HOLD_MS)
  1395	        val commListener = AudioManager.OnCommunicationDeviceChangedListener { device ->
  1396	            val ours = device != null && device.type == heldSinkType && device.productName?.toString().orEmpty() == heldSinkName
  1397	            if (!ours) synchronized(sessionLock) { if (warmHold === hold) hold.end(WarmHold.END_DEVICE_CHANGED) }
  1398	        }
  1399	        val deviceCallback = object : AudioDeviceCallback() {
  1400	            override fun onAudioDevicesRemoved(removed: Array<out AudioDeviceInfo>) {
  1401	                val gone = removed.any { it.type == heldSinkType && it.productName?.toString().orEmpty() == heldSinkName }
  1402	                if (gone) synchronized(sessionLock) { if (warmHold === hold) hold.end(WarmHold.END_DEVICE_REMOVED) }
  1403	            }
  1404	        }
  1405	        holdCommListener = commListener
  1406	        holdDeviceCallback = deviceCallback
  1407	        runCatching { audioManager.addOnCommunicationDeviceChangedListener({ routeHandler.post(it) }, commListener) }
  1408	            .onFailure { DebugLogger.warn(TAG, "hold listener not registered: ${it.message}") }
  1409	        runCatching { audioManager.registerAudioDeviceCallback(deviceCallback, routeHandler) }
  1410	            .onFailure { DebugLogger.warn(TAG, "hold device callback not registered: ${it.message}") }
  1411	        DebugLogger.log(TAG, "route hold start=$label ms=${WarmHold.HOLD_MS}")
  1412	        return true
  1413	    }
  1414	
  1415	    /** Runs inside `WarmHold.end` or `handOver`, under `sessionLock`. */
  1416	    private fun onHoldEnded(reason: String, label: String) {
  1417	        DebugLogger.log(TAG, "route hold end=$reason device=$label")

  1420	        if (reason != WarmHold.END_NEW_TAKE && session == null) stopSelf()

  1422	
  1423	    /** Under `sessionLock`. Forgets the hold's listeners and identity; the hold object itself is done. */
  1424	    private fun clearHoldBookkeeping() {

  1452	                false
  1453	            }
  1454	        }
  1455	    }
  1462	    private class AudioTrackSilence : WarmHold.SilentTrack {
  1463	        private var track: AudioTrack? = null
  1464	        private var thread: Thread? = null
  1465	        @Volatile private var stopped = false
  1466	
  1467	        override fun play() {
  1468	            val rate = PcmAudio.SAMPLE_RATE
  1469	            val minimum = AudioTrack.getMinBufferSize(rate, AudioFormat.CHANNEL_OUT_MONO, AudioFormat.ENCODING_PCM_16BIT)
  1470	            val built = AudioTrack.Builder()
  1471	                .setAudioAttributes(
  1472	                    AudioAttributes.Builder()
  1473	                        .setUsage(AudioAttributes.USAGE_VOICE_COMMUNICATION)
  1474	                        .setContentType(AudioAttributes.CONTENT_TYPE_SPEECH)
  1475	                        .build(),
  1476	                )
  1477	                .setAudioFormat(
  1478	                    AudioFormat.Builder()
  1479	                        .setSampleRate(rate)
  1480	                        .setChannelMask(AudioFormat.CHANNEL_OUT_MONO)
  1481	                        .setEncoding(AudioFormat.ENCODING_PCM_16BIT)
  1482	                        .build(),
  1483	                )
  1484	                .setBufferSizeInBytes(maxOf(minimum, rate * PcmAudio.BYTES_PER_SAMPLE))
  1485	                .setTransferMode(AudioTrack.MODE_STREAM)
  1486	                .build()
  1487	            if (built.state != AudioTrack.STATE_INITIALIZED) {
  1488	                built.release()
  1489	                throw IllegalStateException("silent track not initialized")
  1490	            }
  1491	            track = built
  1492	            built.play()
  1493	            val zeros = ByteArray(rate / 10 * PcmAudio.BYTES_PER_SAMPLE)
  1494	            thread = Thread({
  1495	                while (!stopped) {
  1496	                    val n = built.write(zeros, 0, zeros.size)
  1497	                    if (n < 0) break
  1498	                }
  1499	            }, "WarmHoldSilence").apply { start() }
  1500	        }
  1501	
  1502	        override fun stop() {
  1503	            stopped = true
  1504	            track?.let { t ->
  1505	                runCatching { t.stop() }
  1506	                runCatching { t.release() }
  1507	            }
  1508	            track = null
  1509	        }
  1510	    }
  1511	
  1512	    private fun closeResources(record: AudioRecord?, output: FileOutputStream?) {
  1513	        runCatching { output?.flush() }
  1514	            .onFailure { DebugLogger.warn(TAG, "Failed to flush audio file: ${it.message}") }
  1515	        runCatching { output?.close() }
  1516	            .onFailure { DebugLogger.warn(TAG, "Failed to close audio file: ${it.message}") }
  1517	        runCatching { record?.stop() }
  1518	            .onFailure {
  1519	                if (it !is IllegalStateException) DebugLogger.warn(TAG, "Failed to stop AudioRecord: ${it.message}")
  1520	            }
  1521	        runCatching { record?.release() }
  1522	            .onFailure { DebugLogger.warn(TAG, "Failed to release AudioRecord: ${it.message}") }
  1523	    }
  1524	
  1525	    /** Wait for the capture thread to finish writing and close the file. */
  1526	    fun waitForFileReady(timeoutMs: Long = 2_000L): Boolean {
  1527	        val thread = captureThread
  1528	        if (thread != null && Thread.currentThread() !== thread) {
  1529	            thread.join(timeoutMs.coerceAtLeast(0L))
  1530	        }
  1531	        val ready = thread?.isAlive != true && session == null
  1532	        if (ready) captureThread = null
  1533	        return ready
  1534	    }
  1535	
  1536	    override fun onDestroy() {
  1537	        // Ordered: no hold may start after this flag, so the take stopRecording ends below cannot open
  1538	        // one after the route thread is gone (Codex review 1).
  1539	        destroyed = true
  1540	        synchronized(sessionLock) { warmHold?.end(WarmHold.END_DESTROYED) }
  1541	        stopRecording()
  1542	        session?.let { active ->
  1543	            active.detectorAbandoned.set(true)
  1544	            active.feederThread?.interrupt()
  1545	            active.analyserThread?.interrupt()
  1546	            unbindVad(active)
  1547	        }
  1548	        val thread = captureThread
  1549	        if (thread != null && thread !== Thread.currentThread()) {
  1550	            thread.join(2_000L)
  1551	            if (thread.isAlive) {
  1552	                // The capture thread is the sole owner of AudioRecord and the file. Do not
  1553	                // close either resource here after the bounded wait. Process termination of
  1554	                // the isolated :audio service will reclaim them without an ANR-length wait.
  1555	                DebugLogger.warn(TAG, "Capture thread did not finish during service teardown")
  1556	            }
  1557	        }
  1558	        if (captureThread?.isAlive != true) {
  1559	            captureThread = null
  1560	            synchronized(sessionLock) {
  1561	                isRecording.set(false)
  1562	            }
  1563	        }
  1564	        // A hold that slipped in between the flag and the join is ended here, before its expiry dies.
  1565	        synchronized(sessionLock) { warmHold?.end(WarmHold.END_DESTROYED) }
  1566	        // After the join: the capture thread's cleanup removed its listener; nothing else posts here.
  1567	        routeThread.quitSafely()
  1568	        super.onDestroy()
  1569	    }
  1570	}

## Findings

1. **Medium — REPRODUCIBLE (start any take):** `startedAtMs` is now captured before preferred-device setup, file creation, and `AudioRecord.startRecording()`, while the pre-split service captured it afterward, inflating `getLiveAfterMs()` and route-live timing; `app/src/main/java/com/envi/wispr/audio/AudioCaptureService.kt:379-405`; `app/src/main/java/com/envi/wispr/audio/TakeRoute.kt:45-53`; `2ed2cdf:app/src/main/java/com/envi/wispr/audio/AudioCaptureService.kt:518-549`.

   Fix:

   ```kotlin
   // TakeRoute.kt: remove startedAtMs from the constructor.
   @Volatile
   var startedAtMs: Long = 0L
       private set

   fun markRecorderStarted(atMs: Long) {
       check(startedAtMs == 0L)
       startedAtMs = atMs
   }
   ```

   ```kotlin
   // AudioCaptureService.kt: preserve the original ordering.
   record.startRecording()
   record.routedDevice?.let {
       effective.observe(it.type, it.productName?.toString().orEmpty())
   }
   takeRoute.markRecorderStarted(SystemClock.elapsedRealtime())
   ```

   Remove the unused `CaptureSession.startedAtMs` field and the `startedAtMs` constructor argument in `AudioLimbCloseTest.kt:59`; add this guard to `LiveGateWiringTest.kt`:

   ```kotlin
   val start = body(capture, "private fun startRecording(")
   assertTrue(
       "the recorder starts before its route clock is captured",
       start.indexOf("record.startRecording()") <
           start.indexOf("takeRoute.markRecorderStarted(SystemClock.elapsedRealtime())"),
   )
   ```

2. **Medium — REPRODUCIBLE (finish any auto-stop-enabled take after the detector binds):** `releaseSession` now synchronously calls `unbindService`, whereas the pre-split release only abandoned and interrupted the feeder and let its `finally` unbind; additionally, the volatile check-then-set allows `close()` and the feeder’s `finally` to unbind the same connection concurrently; `app/src/main/java/com/envi/wispr/audio/AudioCaptureService.kt:669-691,737-746`; `app/src/main/java/com/envi/wispr/audio/DetectorFeed.kt:77-81,296-299,326-346`; `2ed2cdf:app/src/main/java/com/envi/wispr/audio/AudioCaptureService.kt:1195-1198,1332-1338,1542-1546`.

   Fix:

   ```kotlin
   // DetectorFeed.kt
   private val vadBound = AtomicBoolean(false)

   // In start():
   val bound = runCatching { bind(connection) }.getOrDefault(false)
   vadBound.set(bound)
   if (!bound) {
       vadConnection = null
       abandon()
       DebugLogger.warn(tag, "Auto-stop unavailable: detector service could not be bound")
       return
   }

   private fun unbindVad() {
       if (!vadBound.compareAndSet(true, false)) return
       val connection = vadConnection ?: return
       vadConnection = null
       vadService = null
       runCatching { unbind(connection) }
           .onFailure { DebugLogger.warn(tag, "Detector unbind failed: ${it.message}") }
   }

   fun close(forceUnbind: Boolean = false) {
       if (closed.compareAndSet(false, true)) {
           detectorAbandoned.set(true)
           feederThread?.let { runCatching { interrupt(it) } }
       }
       if (forceUnbind) unbindVad()
   }
   ```

   ```kotlin
   // AudioCaptureService.kt
   // releaseSession:
   active.detector.close()

   // onDestroy:
   active.detector.close(forceUnbind = true)
   ```

   Update `AudioLimbCloseTest.kt:91-94` to call `close(forceUnbind = true)` twice, and update the destruction assertion in `AudioServiceShapeTest.kt:43-45` to require that exact call.

3. **Low — HYPOTHETICAL:** `theServiceKeepsOnlyTheSessionAndTheBinder` checks only historical member names, so an equivalent member renamed from `warmHold` or `effective` passes; `app/src/test/java/com/envi/wispr/audio/AudioServiceShapeTest.kt:55-85`.

   Replace the name-by-name moved-member checks with complete outer-service allowlists:

   ```kotlin
   val expectedFunctions = setOf(
       "nextCaptureToken",
       "startRecording",
       "captureLoop",
       "claimEnding",
       "stopRecording",
       "endTake",
       "endTakeLocked",
       "releaseSession",
       "closeResources",
       "waitForFileReady",
   )
   val actualFunctions = Regex(
       """^ {4}(?:(?:private|internal|public|protected|inline|suspend|operator|tailrec|infix)\s+)*fun\s+(\w+)\s*\(""",
       RegexOption.MULTILINE,
   ).findAll(service).map { it.groupValues[1] }.toSet()
   assertEquals("the service has only its allowed non-override functions", expectedFunctions, actualFunctions)

   val expectedFields = setOf(
       "sessionLock",
       "session",
       "lastEffective",
       "lastStartFailure",
       "warmHoldOwner",
       "destroyed",
       "routeThread",
       "routeHandler",
       "routeScheduler",
       "isRecording",
       "captureThread",
       "lastAudioFile",
       "currentAmplitude",
       "spectrumListener",
       "takePeakAmplitude",
       "lastSilenceStatus",
       "terminalReason",
       "tokens",
       "binder",
   )
   val actualFields = Regex(
       """^ {4}(?:@\w+(?:\([^)]*\))?\s+)*(?:(?:private|internal|public|protected|lateinit|const)\s+)*(?:val|var)\s+(\w+)\b""",
       RegexOption.MULTILINE,
   ).findAll(service).map { it.groupValues[1] }.toSet()
   assertEquals("the service has only its allowed fields", expectedFields, actualFields)
   ```

4. **Low — HYPOTHETICAL:** the new buffer constant is public even though every reader is inside the app Gradle module; `app/src/main/java/com/envi/wispr/audio/PcmAudio.kt:23`.

   Fix:

   ```kotlin
   internal const val READ_CHUNK_BYTES = 1_024
   ```

## Axes

- **A1 — FOUND:** recorder-start clock ordering changed, and detector unbinding moved from feeder-finally to synchronous release; `AudioCaptureService.kt:379-405,669-691`; `DetectorFeed.kt:296-299,342-346`.
- **A2 — NOT FOUND:** handover, eligibility, hold start, binder finish, started-service check, and both destruction closes retain `sessionLock`; `AudioCaptureService.kt:232,297-299,320-335,671-680,737-764`; `WarmHoldOwner.kt:49-74,112-158`.
- **A3 — NOT FOUND:** the capture loop and synchronous owner calls add no new lock, sleep, logging, allocation, or binder transaction; the `markLive` log remains inside the posted route callback; `AudioCaptureService.kt:515-593`; `DetectorFeed.kt:101-123`; `PicturePublisher.kt:51-59`; `TakeRoute.kt:211-212,245-257`.
- **A4 — FOUND:** `DetectorFeed`’s non-atomic `vadBound` permits close/finally double-unbind; other owner close flags and per-owner thread fields do not cross into a later take; `DetectorFeed.kt:77-81,296-299,326-346`; `PicturePublisher.kt:41-42,86-90,130-132`; `TakeRoute.kt:153-157,326-343`.
- **A5 — NOT FOUND:** every service lambda reads the live `session` slot when invoked, and `stillWaiting` also reads `isRecording` and the gate; `AudioCaptureService.kt:455-489`.
- **A6 — NOT FOUND:** hold creation precedes route close, successful holding releases only the listener, failed track startup returns `false` after releasing the route, and non-holding closes the whole `RouteHold`; `AudioCaptureService.kt:671-680,708-710`; `WarmHoldOwner.kt:73-109`; `TakeRoute.kt:333-343`.
- **A7 — FOUND:** repointed behavioral assertions remain meaningful, but the service-shape guard is rename-evadable; `SilenceStopWiringTest.kt:74-159`; `LiveGateWiringTest.kt:35-152`; `LiveAudioMeterWiringTest.kt:103-210`; `AudioServiceShapeTest.kt:55-85`.

## Findings

1. **Medium — REPRODUCIBLE (start any take):** `startedAtMs` is now captured before preferred-device setup, file creation, and `AudioRecord.startRecording()`, while the pre-split service captured it afterward, inflating `getLiveAfterMs()` and route-live timing; `app/src/main/java/com/envi/wispr/audio/AudioCaptureService.kt:379-405`; `app/src/main/java/com/envi/wispr/audio/TakeRoute.kt:45-53`; `2ed2cdf:app/src/main/java/com/envi/wispr/audio/AudioCaptureService.kt:518-549`.

   Fix:

   ```kotlin
   // TakeRoute.kt: remove startedAtMs from the constructor.
   @Volatile
   var startedAtMs: Long = 0L
       private set

   fun markRecorderStarted(atMs: Long) {
       check(startedAtMs == 0L)
       startedAtMs = atMs
   }
   ```

   ```kotlin
   // AudioCaptureService.kt: preserve the original ordering.
   record.startRecording()
   record.routedDevice?.let {
       effective.observe(it.type, it.productName?.toString().orEmpty())
   }
   takeRoute.markRecorderStarted(SystemClock.elapsedRealtime())
   ```

   Remove the unused `CaptureSession.startedAtMs` field and the `startedAtMs` constructor argument in `AudioLimbCloseTest.kt:59`; add this guard to `LiveGateWiringTest.kt`:

   ```kotlin
   val start = body(capture, "private fun startRecording(")
   assertTrue(
       "the recorder starts before its route clock is captured",
       start.indexOf("record.startRecording()") <
           start.indexOf("takeRoute.markRecorderStarted(SystemClock.elapsedRealtime())"),
   )
   ```

2. **Medium — REPRODUCIBLE (finish any auto-stop-enabled take after the detector binds):** `releaseSession` now synchronously calls `unbindService`, whereas the pre-split release only abandoned and interrupted the feeder and let its `finally` unbind; additionally, the volatile check-then-set allows `close()` and the feeder’s `finally` to unbind the same connection concurrently; `app/src/main/java/com/envi/wispr/audio/AudioCaptureService.kt:669-691,737-746`; `app/src/main/java/com/envi/wispr/audio/DetectorFeed.kt:77-81,296-299,326-346`; `2ed2cdf:app/src/main/java/com/envi/wispr/audio/AudioCaptureService.kt:1195-1198,1332-1338,1542-1546`.

   Fix:

   ```kotlin
   // DetectorFeed.kt
   private val vadBound = AtomicBoolean(false)

   // In start():
   val bound = runCatching { bind(connection) }.getOrDefault(false)
   vadBound.set(bound)
   if (!bound) {
       vadConnection = null
       abandon()
       DebugLogger.warn(tag, "Auto-stop unavailable: detector service could not be bound")
       return
   }

   private fun unbindVad() {
       if (!vadBound.compareAndSet(true, false)) return
       val connection = vadConnection ?: return
       vadConnection = null
       vadService = null
       runCatching { unbind(connection) }
           .onFailure { DebugLogger.warn(tag, "Detector unbind failed: ${it.message}") }
   }

   fun close(forceUnbind: Boolean = false) {
       if (closed.compareAndSet(false, true)) {
           detectorAbandoned.set(true)
           feederThread?.let { runCatching { interrupt(it) } }
       }
       if (forceUnbind) unbindVad()
   }
   ```

   ```kotlin
   // AudioCaptureService.kt
   // releaseSession:
   active.detector.close()

   // onDestroy:
   active.detector.close(forceUnbind = true)
   ```

   Update `AudioLimbCloseTest.kt:91-94` to call `close(forceUnbind = true)` twice, and update the destruction assertion in `AudioServiceShapeTest.kt:43-45` to require that exact call.

3. **Low — HYPOTHETICAL:** `theServiceKeepsOnlyTheSessionAndTheBinder` checks only historical member names, so an equivalent member renamed from `warmHold` or `effective` passes; `app/src/test/java/com/envi/wispr/audio/AudioServiceShapeTest.kt:55-85`.

   Replace the name-by-name moved-member checks with complete outer-service allowlists:

   ```kotlin
   val expectedFunctions = setOf(
       "nextCaptureToken",
       "startRecording",
       "captureLoop",
       "claimEnding",
       "stopRecording",
       "endTake",
       "endTakeLocked",
       "releaseSession",
       "closeResources",
       "waitForFileReady",
   )
   val actualFunctions = Regex(
       """^ {4}(?:(?:private|internal|public|protected|inline|suspend|operator|tailrec|infix)\s+)*fun\s+(\w+)\s*\(""",
       RegexOption.MULTILINE,
   ).findAll(service).map { it.groupValues[1] }.toSet()
   assertEquals("the service has only its allowed non-override functions", expectedFunctions, actualFunctions)

   val expectedFields = setOf(
       "sessionLock",
       "session",
       "lastEffective",
       "lastStartFailure",
       "warmHoldOwner",
       "destroyed",
       "routeThread",
       "routeHandler",
       "routeScheduler",
       "isRecording",
       "captureThread",
       "lastAudioFile",
       "currentAmplitude",
       "spectrumListener",
       "takePeakAmplitude",
       "lastSilenceStatus",
       "terminalReason",
       "tokens",
       "binder",
   )
   val actualFields = Regex(
       """^ {4}(?:@\w+(?:\([^)]*\))?\s+)*(?:(?:private|internal|public|protected|lateinit|const)\s+)*(?:val|var)\s+(\w+)\b""",
       RegexOption.MULTILINE,
   ).findAll(service).map { it.groupValues[1] }.toSet()
   assertEquals("the service has only its allowed fields", expectedFields, actualFields)
   ```

4. **Low — HYPOTHETICAL:** the new buffer constant is public even though every reader is inside the app Gradle module; `app/src/main/java/com/envi/wispr/audio/PcmAudio.kt:23`.

   Fix:

   ```kotlin
   internal const val READ_CHUNK_BYTES = 1_024
   ```
